




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

abhidhammapiṭakai

paṭṭhānapāḷi

(pañ‍camau bhāgau)

dhammānulaumai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

1. sukhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. kusalaṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ…pai॰… sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

3. kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau kusalassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau akusalassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau  sukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttau dhammau abyākatassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau kusalassa sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. dukkhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

5. akusalaṃ  dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

6. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|) (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

7. akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau akusalassa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau akusalassa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau akusalassa dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

8. haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3. adukkhamasukhavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

9. kusalaṃ  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

10. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ)|

11. kusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau kusalassa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau akusalassa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

abyākatau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau abyākatassa adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

12. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā satta, anantarai satta…pai॰… upanissayai nava, avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1-2. kusalattika-vipākattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

13. abyākataṃ  vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

14. kusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā dvai, ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

15. kusalau vipākadhammadhammau kusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

akusalau vipākadhammadhammau akusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

kusalau vipākadhammadhammau kusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau vipākadhammadhammau akusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

akusalau vipākadhammadhammau akusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau vipākadhammadhammau kusalassa vipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

16. haituyā  dvai, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā tīṇi, anantarai dvai…pai॰… sahajātai dvai, upanissayai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ| yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

17. abyākataṃ  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

1-3. kusalattika-upādin‍nattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

18. abyākataṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha aikaṃ|)

19. kusalaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

kusalaṃ  anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca abyākataṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca abyākataṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

20. haituyā  nava, avigatai nava| (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

21. kusalau  anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau kusalassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

akusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau akusalassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau abyākatassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

22. kusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau kusalassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

akusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau akusalassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau abyākatassa anupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

23. haituyā satta, ārammaṇai nava, avigatai aikādasa| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

24. kusalaṃ  anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

26. kusalaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

kusalaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

29. kusalaṃ  savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

akusalaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ  savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi, avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

32. kusalaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

abyākataṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā  dvai, avigatai dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraipi pañhāvāraipi sabbattha vitthārau|)

33. kusalaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

kusalaṃ avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca abyākataṃ avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

132. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (aikaṃ)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (dvai)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (tīṇi)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca anidassanaappaṭighau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cattāri)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca anidassanaappaṭighau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (pañ‍ca)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca anidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (cha)| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca anidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau ca anidassanaappaṭighau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (satta)|

133. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (satta)| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ abyākatañ‍ca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (satta, saṃkhittaṃ)|

1-1. haituduka-sahaitukadukaṃ

sahaitukapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. haituṃ  sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

2. nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

3. haituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

6. haitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa sahaitukassa ca nahaitussa sahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

7. haitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa sahaitukassa ca nahaitussa sahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu  sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa  sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau haitussa sahaitukassa ca nahaitussa sahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

haitu  sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā haitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā nahaitussa sahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā haitussa sahaitukassa ca nahaitussa sahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

ahaitukapadaṃ

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

9. haituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ ahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ ahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai aikaṃ, avigatai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

10. haitu ahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haitu  ahaitukau dhammau haitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu ahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau haitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2-1. sahaitukaduka-haitudukaṃ

68. haitusampayuttaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitusampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

haituyā nava|

4-1. haitusahaitukaduka-haitudukaṃ

69. haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva sahaitukau ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

5-1. haituhaitusampayuttaduka-haitudukaṃ

70. haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

haitusampayuttañ‍caiva  na ca haituṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitu nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

saraṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… araṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

araṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

(sabbattha vitthārau|)

dhammānulaumai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

anulaumapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1. kusalattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

1. nakusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ abyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalā abyākatā tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

2. naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

3. naabyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| cha|

4. nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

5. naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

6. nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

7. haituyā  aikūnatiṃsa, ārammaṇai catuvīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa|

(sahajātavārampi  pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

1. kusalattikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

8. nakusalau dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| nakusalau  dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naabyākatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau naakusalassa ca naabyākatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cha|

naakusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| cha|

naabyākatau dhammau naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| cha|

nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| cha|

naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| cha|

nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| cha|

9. nakusalau  dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa adhipatipac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… añ‍ñamañ‍ñapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… nakusalau dhammau nakusalassa  ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cha|

naakusalau  dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naakusalau dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… naakusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cha|

nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā naakusalassa dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

10. haituyā aikūnatiṃsa, ārammaṇai chattiṃsa, adhipatiyā pañ‍catiṃsa, anantarai catuttiṃsa, samanantarai catuttiṃsa, sahajātai aikūnatiṃsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai catuvīsa, nissayai catuttiṃsa, upanissayai chattiṃsa, puraijātai aṭṭhārasa, pacchājātai aṭṭhārasa, āsaivanai catuvīsa, kammai aikūnatiṃsa, vipākai nava, āhārai aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai catuttiṃsa|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

2. vaidanāttikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

11. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

20. ajjhattattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

29. naajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nabahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

21. ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

30. naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

22. sanidassanattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

31. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

32. naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

33. naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca  naanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

34. haituyā tiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa|

(yathā kusalattikai sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi pañhāvārampi gaṇitaṃ aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1. haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. nahaituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaituṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (yāva mahābhūtā)| nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca na nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaitū khandhai paṭic‍ca haitū; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ca na nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

na nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca na nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| na nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| na nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nahaitu ca na nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituñ‍ca na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍ca na  nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca na nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍ca na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ca na nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. na  nahaitu dhammau na nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| na nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| na nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa ca na nahaitussa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu dhammau na nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa ca na nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

na nahaitu dhammau na nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| na nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| na nahaitu dhammau nahaitussa ca na nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu ca na nahaitu ca dhammā nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu  ca na nahaitu ca dhammā na nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu ca na nahaitu ca dhammā nahaitussa ca na nahaitussa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. sahaitukadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

5. nasahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ kaṭattārūpaṃ upādārūpaṃ| nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukā khandhā| nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau ca naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau ca naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

6. haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

7. nasahaitukau dhammau nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasahaitukau dhammau naahaitukassa  dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasahaitukau dhammau nasahaitukassa ca naahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

naahaitukau dhammau naahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nasahaitukau dhammau nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ|)

8. haituyā cha, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3. haitusampayuttadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

9. nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau ca nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau ca nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaitusampayuttañ‍ca nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

10. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

11. nahaituñ‍caiva  naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva na nahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naahaitukau caiva na nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naahaitukañ‍caiva na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva na nahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukañ‍caiva na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukañ‍caiva na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naahaitukau caiva na nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍caiva na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva na nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

6. nahaitusahaitukadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau ca nahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nahaitu nasahaitukau ca nahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa naahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

21. nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappaṭighau ca naappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

22. nasappaṭighau  dhammau nasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasappaṭighau dhammau naappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasappaṭighau dhammau nasappaṭighassa ca naappaṭighassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

24. narūpiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī ca naarūpī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī ca naarūpī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

narūpiñ‍ca naarūpiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| narūpiñ‍ca naarūpiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| narūpiñ‍ca naarūpiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī ca naarūpī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

25. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi sabbattha vitthārau|)

26. ganthadukaṃ

40. nauganthaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau ca nanauganthau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau ca nanauganthau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nauganthañ‍ca  nanauganthañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauganthañ‍ca nanauganthañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauganthau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauganthañ‍ca nanauganthañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau ca nanauganthau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

28. ganthasampayuttadukaṃ

42. naganthasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau ca naganthavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naganthavippayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthavippayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau ca naganthavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naganthasampayuttañ‍ca naganthavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthasampayuttañ‍ca naganthavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| naganthasampayuttañ‍ca naganthavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthasampayuttau ca naganthavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

29. ganthaganthaniyadukaṃ

43. naganthañ‍caiva naaganthaniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthau caiva naaganthaniyau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthañ‍caiva naaganthaniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaganthaniyau caiva nanau ca ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naganthañ‍caiva naaganthaniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naganthau caiva naaganthaniyau ca naaganthaniyau caiva nanau ca ganthau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

50-54. parāmāsādidukāni

50. naparāmāsasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparāmāsasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

51. naparāmāsañ‍caiva naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparāmāsau caiva naaparāmaṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naparāmāsañ‍caiva naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaparāmaṭṭhau caiva nanau ca parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naparāmāsañ‍caiva naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naparāmāsau  caiva naaparāmaṭṭhau ca naaparāmaṭṭhau caiva nanauparāmāsau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nanau ca parāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparāmāsau caiva naaparāmaṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naparāmāsañ‍caiva naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍ca naaparāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nanau ca parāmāsañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparāmāsau caiva naaparāmaṭṭhau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

naasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituyā pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

dhammapac‍canīyai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyai dukatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. haituduka-kusalattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. nahaituṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaituṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ| nahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaituṃ nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaitū akusalai abyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca haitū| nahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau ca nanahaitu nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau ca nanahaitu nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ  nakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau ca nanahaitu nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

2. haituyā  nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

nahaituyā dvai|

(sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha vitthārau|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau nanahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau nahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau nahaitussa nakusalassa ca nanahaitussa nakusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu nakusalau dhammau nahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

5. nahaituṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaituṃ kusalaṃ abyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… dvai khandhai…pai॰… nahaituṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – nahaitū kusalai abyākatai khandhai paṭic‍ca haitū| nahaituṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau ca nanahaitu naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu  naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau ca nanahaitu naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau ca nanahaitu naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sabbattha nava|)

6. nahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalākusalaṃ nahaituṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā, tayau khandhai paṭic‍ca aikau khandhau| nahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naabyākatau ca nanahaitu naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca  nanahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naabyākatau ca nanahaitu naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sabbattha nava|)

haitupac‍cayau

29. nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca nanahaitu nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

31. nanahaitu  naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nanahaitussa naanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nanahaitu naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nahaitussa naanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nanahaitu naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nahaitussa naanidassanasappaṭighassa ca nanahaitussa naanidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nahaitussa naanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… upanissayai nava, puraijātai pacchājātai tīṇi, āsaivanai nava, kammai tīṇi, vipākai nava, āhārai tīṇi, indriyai nava, jhānai tīṇi, maggai sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai pañ‍ca…pai॰… avigatai nava| (pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2-3-1. sahaitukadukādi-kusalattikaṃ

33. nasahaitukaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  – vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ, aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai…pai॰… nasahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casahagataṃ mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā; paṭisandhikkhaṇai vatthuṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukā khandhā| nasahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nakusalau ca naahaitukau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nasahaitukaṃ  nakusalañ‍ca naahaitukaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava|

1. nakusalaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau  nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

2. naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

3. naabyākataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| cha|

4. nakusalaṃ  nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

5. naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

2-1. vaidanāttika-haitudukaṃ

52. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

22-1-6. sanidassanattika-haitvādidukāni

69. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā  tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

70. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (nava)|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

73. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaṅkhataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

74. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

75. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

76. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

77. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

78. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

22-14-54. sanidassanattika-āsavādigaucchakāni

22-55-68. sanidassanattika-mahantaradukāni

83. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

84. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

85. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca …pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

22-69-82. sanidassanattika-upādānādidukāni

89. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauupādānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

22-83. sanidassanattika-piṭṭhidukaṃ

90. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

haituyā tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… añ‍ñamañ‍ñai pañ‍cavīsa…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa| (piṭṭhidukaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

91. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… cha|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… cha|

naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

haituyā tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

92. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

93. naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

94. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sahajātavāraipi pac‍cayavāraipi nissayavāraipi saṃsaṭṭhavāraipi sampayuttavāraipi sabbattha nava|)

dhammapac‍canīyai tikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

haitupac‍cayau

1. nakusalaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

2. naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

3. naabyākataṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

4. nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

5. naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

6. nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa, ārammaṇai catuvīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sahajātavāraipi…pai॰… pañhāvāraipi vitthārau|)

dukkhāyavaidanāyasampayuttapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

7. nakusalaṃ  nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naabyākatau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naabyākataṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

tatiyapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

8. nakusalaṃ  naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

1-2. kusalattika-vipākattikaṃ

9. nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau ca naabyākatau navipākau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau ca naakusalau navipākau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naabyākataṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca naabyākataṃ navipākañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca naabyākataṃ navipākañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca naakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

10. nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naakusalaṃ  navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naakusalaṃ  navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ)|

haituyā  nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sabbattha nava|)

1-21. kusalattika-sanidassanattikaṃ

36. nakusalaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naabyākatau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau  ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

nakusalaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naakusalau nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

37. nakusalaṃ  naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnatiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnatiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

38. nakusalaṃ  naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava…pai॰… (sabbattha vitthārau)|

2-1. vaidanāttika-kusalattikaṃ

39. nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau  ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

40. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnapañ‍ñāsa, ārammaṇai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa|

41. nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā aikūnapañ‍ñāsa|

42. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā  aṭṭhacattālīsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

2-2. vaidanāttika-vipākattikaṃ

43. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnapañ‍ñāsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

3-1. vipākattika-kusalattikaṃ

44. navipākaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… navipākadhammadhammaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

4-1. upādin‍nattika-kusalattikaṃ

45. naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nakusalaṃ itau paṭṭhāya yāva ajjhattārammaṇattikā sabbapautthakaisu akusalābyākatapadāni na dissanti, vaidanāttika sanidassanattikaisu viya nanu taihipi bhavitabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naanupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-kusalattikaṃ

46. nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

6-21-1. vitak‍kādittikāni-kusalattikaṃ

47. nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

48. napītisahagataṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasukhasahagataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naupaikkhāsahagataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

49. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

50. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

51. naācayagāmiṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naapacayagāmiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

52. nasaikkhaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naasaikkhaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nanaivāsaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

53. naparittaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… namahaggataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naappamāṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

54. naparittārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… namahaggatārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naappamāṇārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

55. nahīnaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… namajjhimaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… napaṇītaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

56. namicchattaniyataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasammattaniyataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naaniyataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

57. namaggārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… namaggahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… namaggādhipatiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

58. naanuppan‍naṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nauppādiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

59. naatītaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naanāgataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

60. naatītārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… naanāgatārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

61. naajjhattaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nabahiddhā nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

62. naajjhattārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

22-1. sanidassanattika-kusalattikaṃ

63. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… cha|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… cha|

naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha (saṃkhittaṃ)| haituyā tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

64. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

65. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

haituyā| nava (sabbattha nava|)

22-2-21. sanidassanattika-vaidanādittikāni

66. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|
