nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|

67. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā nava|

68. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|

69. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… haituyā tiṃsa|

70. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

71. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

72. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

73. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

74. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naapacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

75. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

76. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

77. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naparittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰…|

80. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namaggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namaggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

81. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanuppan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauppādiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

82. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naatītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanāgataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

83. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

84. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

85. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

haituyā tiṃsa…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

86. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… cha|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabahiddhārammaṇañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tiṃsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tiṃsa|

(sahajātavāraipi pac‍cayavāraipi nissayavāraipi saṃsaṭṭhavāraipi sampayuttavāraipi pañhāvāraipi vitthārau|)

dhammapac‍canīyai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1-5. haituduka-sahaitukādidukāni

1. nahaituṃ  nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

2. nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau  uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi|

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. nanahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau nanahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai cattāri|

4. nahaituṃ  naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau ca nanahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau ca nanahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ  naahaitukañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau ca nanahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sabbattha nava|)

5. nahaituṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

6. nahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nanahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituyā nava| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

7. nahaituṃ  nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (yāva pañhāvāraipi aikaṃ|)

nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva nana ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau caiva nana ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nahaituṃ  nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nanahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva nana ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nahaituvippayuttau caiva nana ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sabbattha aikaṃ|)

1-6. haituduka-cūḷantaradukaṃ

8. nahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau ca nanahaitu naappac‍cayau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau ca nanahaitu  naappac‍cayau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ  naappac‍cayañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naappac‍cayañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naappac‍cayañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naappac‍cayañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappac‍cayau ca nanahaitu naappac‍cayau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)|

haituyā  nava|

nahaituṃ naasaṅkhataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nahaituṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nahaituṃ nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

1-13-18. haituduka-āsavādigaucchakāni

9. nahaituṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… nahaituṃ naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naāsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naāsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ nauāsavañ‍caiva  naanāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naanāsavañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ  naāsavañ‍caiva naāsavavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

nahaituṃ naāsavavippayuttañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…|

2-6-1. sahaitukādidukāni-haitudukaṃ

18. nasahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu ca naahaitukau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naahaitukaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu ca naahaitukau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nasahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu ca naahaitukau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pac‍canīyapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1. kusalattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. kusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| (5)

2. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalai khandhai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – akusalaṃ aikaṃ  khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

3. abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… aikaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau mahābhūtā…pai॰… dvai mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca dvai mahābhūtā, mahābhūtai paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ…pai॰… paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… mahābhūtaṃ…pai॰… abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā – vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ…pai॰…| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – kusalai khandhai ca mahābhūtai ca paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

akusalañ‍ca  abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalañ‍ca abyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (cittasamuṭṭhānarūpamaiva aittha vattati, aikūnavīsati pañhā kātabbā|)

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

5. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| abyākataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti ārammaṇapac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnavīsa, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikūnavīsa, anantarai nava, samanantarai nava, sahajātai aikūnavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnavīsa|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

6. akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā| (3)

abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā| (3)

7. kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā cha, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa, naadhipatiyā aikūnavīsa…pai॰… nauvigatai pan‍narasa|

(pac‍canīyaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ| pac‍cayavāraipi  haituyā chabbīsa, ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa…pai॰… avigatai chabbīsa| nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

1. kusalattikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

8. kusalau  dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau naabyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau naakusalassa ca naabyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

9. akusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau naabyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naabyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau dhammau nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

abyākatau  dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau dhammau nakusalassa ca naakusalassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

10. kusalau dhammau nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (cha pañhā|)

akusalau dhammau naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau …| (cha pañhā|)

abyākatau dhammau naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…| (cha pañhā, saṃkhittaṃ|)

11. haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai sauḷasa, samanantarai sauḷasa, sahajātai aikūnavīsa, añ‍ñamañ‍ñai nava, nissayai chabbīsa, upanissayai aṭṭhārasa, puraijātai cha, pacchājātai nava, āsaivanai nava, kammai tairasa, vipākai tīṇi, āhārai tairasa…pai॰… maggai tairasa, sampayuttai nava, vippayuttai dvādasa…pai॰… avigatai chabbīsa| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. vaidanāttikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

12. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttai khandhai paṭic‍ca sukhavaidanā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… (mahābhūtā natthi)| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

13. dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā  uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

14. adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikavīsa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

15. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā aikavīsa, naārammaṇai aikavīsa…pai॰… navippayuttai cuddasa…pai॰… nauvigatai aikavīsa|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

2. vaidanāttikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

haitupac‍cayau

16. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  dhammau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nadukkhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttassa ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (7) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikavīsa, ārammaṇai aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3. vipākattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

17. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (pañ‍ca pañhā|)

18. vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (pañ‍ca pañhā|)

19. naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (pañ‍ca pañhā|)

20. vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau ca navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

21. vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… (pañ‍ca pañhā)|

vipākañ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

22. haituyā  taivīsa, ārammaṇai cuddasa…pai॰… avigatai taivīsa| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā aṭṭhārasa, naārammaṇai pan‍narasa|

(sahajātavārampi  pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

23. vipākau dhammau navipākassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākadhammadhammassa ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākassa ca navipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

24. vipākau  dhammau navipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākassa ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākadhammadhammassa ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākau dhammau navipākassa ca navipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

vipākadhammadhammau navipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau navipākassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau navipākassa ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau navipākadhammadhammassa ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vipākadhammadhammau navipākassa ca navipākadhammadhammassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

25. haituyā  tairasa, ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa, adhipatiyā sattarasa, anantarai sauḷasa…pai॰… puraijātai cha, pacchājātai nava, āsaivanai cha, kammai cuddasa, vipākai pañ‍ca, indriyai aṭṭhārasa…pai॰… vippayuttai dvādasa…pai॰… avigatai chabbīsa| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

4. upādin‍nattikaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

26. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau  ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

27. haituyā aikūnavīsa, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikādasa…pai॰… sahajātai aikūnavīsa| (sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

28. upādin‍nupādāniyau  dhammau naupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

29. upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍nupādāniyau  dhammau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍nupādāniyau  dhammau naupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

5. saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

31. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca  asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnavīsa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai aikūnavīsa| (sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ| pañhāvārai na sadisaṃ|)

32. haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai pan‍narasa, adhipatiyā pan‍narasa, anantarai sauḷasa…pai॰… puraijātai cha, pacchājātai nava, āsaivanai aṭṭha, kammai tairasa, vipākai aṭṭha, āhārai tairasa…pai॰… maggai tairasa, vippayuttai dvādasa…pai॰… avigatai chabbīsa|

6. vitak‍kattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

33. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

34. avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau ca naavitak‍kaavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

savitak‍kasavicārañ‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattañ‍ca avitak‍kaavicārañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnapañ‍ñāsa, ārammaṇai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa…pai॰… avigatai aikūnapañ‍ñāsa| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pītittikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

35. pītisahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau ca naupaikkhāsahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau ca naupaikkhāsahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau ca nasukhasahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau ca nasukhasahagatau ca naupaikkhāsahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (7)

sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

upaikkhāsahagataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta|

pītisahagatañ‍ca sukhasahagatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aṭṭhavīsa, ārammaṇai catuvīsa…pai॰… avigatai aṭṭhavīsa|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi pañhāvārampi sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

36. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naivadassanaina  nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

dassanaina pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

bhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

10. ācayagāmittikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

38. ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī ca nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca naapacayagāmī  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca naapacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca naapacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ācayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca naapacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apacayagāmiñ‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī ca naapacayagāmī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnavīsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

11. saikkhattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

39. saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau ca nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca naasaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

asaikkhaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca naasaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca naasaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

saikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca naasaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

asaikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| asaikkhañ‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau ca naasaikkhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnavīsa| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

12. parittattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

40. parittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau ca namahaggatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca appamāṇañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| parittañ‍ca mahaggatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau ca naappamāṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā taivīsa, ārammaṇai cuddasa| (sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haituyā aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa|

14. hīnattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

15. micchattattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

43. micchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasammattaniyatau ca naaniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau ca nasammattaniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau ca naaniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau ca nasammattaniyatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

aniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

micchattaniyatañ‍ca aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sammattaniyatañ‍ca  aniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikūnavīsa| (sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

22. sanidassanattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

50. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (6)

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

anidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aṭṭhārasa, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai aṭṭhārasa| (sabbattha vitthārau| sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

51. anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau naanidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau naanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anidassanaappaṭighau  dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa ca naanidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau naanidassanasappaṭighassa ca naanidassanaappaṭighassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa ca naanidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (6)

sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

52. haituyā cha, ārammaṇai nava| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1. haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. pac‍cayānulaumaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. haituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā – haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sampayuttakā khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ; paṭisandhikkhaṇai…pai॰… haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ca nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ca nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haituñ‍ca nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍ca nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍ca nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ca nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

2. haitu  dhammau nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu dhammau nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu dhammau nanahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu ca nahaitu ca dhammā nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

3. haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (pañhāvārampi aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. sahaitukadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

4. sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau ca naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau ca naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

sahaitukañ‍ca ahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukañ‍ca  ahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukañ‍ca ahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau ca naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3. haitusampayuttadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

5. haitusampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau ca nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau ca nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haitusampayuttañ‍ca haituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haitusampayuttañ‍ca haituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haitusampayuttañ‍ca haituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau ca nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haitusahaitukadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

6. haituñ‍caiva  sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva  nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

12. laukiyadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

14. laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukiyau ca nalaukuttarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā|) ayaṃ saṅkhyā vicāraitabbā, nalaukiyanalaukuttaradhammau nāma natthi (3)

laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā pañ‍ca| (sabbattha pañ‍ca|)

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

16. āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau ca nanauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau ca nanauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

99. sauttaradukaṃ

51. sauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasauttarau ca naanuttarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

sauttarañ‍ca  anuttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) haituyā pañ‍ca|

100. saraṇadukaṃ

1-6. paṭic‍cavārādi

52. saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau ca naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

saraṇañ‍ca araṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

53. saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituyā dvai, na ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

100. saraṇadukaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

54. saraṇau dhammau nasaraṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau naaraṇassa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau nasaraṇassa ca naaraṇassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

araṇau dhammau nasaraṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

55. saraṇau  dhammau nasaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| saraṇau dhammau naaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri, adhipatiyā pañ‍ca, anantarai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

56. saraṇau  dhammau nasaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

saraṇau dhammau naaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

57. nahaituyā satta, naārammaṇai satta, naadhipatiyā satta, naanantarai satta…pai॰… nauavigatai cattāri|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cattāri| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cattāri| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. haituduka-kusalattikaṃ

1. kusalapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. haituṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ kusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (cittasamuṭṭhānamaiva, ārammaṇaṃ natthi)|

haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… nissayavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

2. haitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu kusalau dhammau nanahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu  kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā nahaitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

3. haituyā  aikaṃ, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi| (pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. akusalapadaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

4. haituṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ akusalañ‍ca nahaituṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… nissayavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

5. haitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa naakusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haitu akusalau dhammau nahaitussa naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu akusalau dhammau nanahaitussa naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

haitu akusalau ca nahaitu akusalau ca dhammā nahaitussa naakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

6. haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

3. abyākatapadaṃ

3. pac‍cayavārau

7. nahaituṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (nissayavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

1-2. haituduka-vaidanāttikaṃ

8. haituṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

9. haituṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

10. haituṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi| (sabbattha vitthārau|)

1-3. haituduka-vipākattikaṃ

11. haituṃ  vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-4. haituduka-upādin‍nattikaṃ

12. haituṃ  upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

haituṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-5. haituduka-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

13. haituṃ saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ  asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-6. haituduka-vitak‍kattikaṃ

14. haituṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-7. haituduka-pītittikaṃ

15. haituṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-8. haituduka-dassanattikaṃ

16. haituṃ  dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-9. haituduka-dassanahaituttikaṃ

17. haituṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

1-10. haituduka-ācayagāmittikaṃ

18. haituṃ ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-11. haituduka-saikkhattikaṃ

19. haituṃ saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ  asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-12. haituduka-parittattikaṃ

20. nahaituṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ appamāṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-13. haituduka-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

21. haituṃ parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naparittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-14. haituduka-hīnattikaṃ

22. haituṃ hīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu namajjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ paṇītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu napaṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-15. haituduka-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

23. haituṃ  micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … (tīṇi)|

nahaituṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-16. haituduka-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

24. haituṃ maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namaggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namaggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu namaggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-17. haituduka-uppan‍nattikaṃ

25. haitu anuppan‍nau dhammau nahaitussa naanuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā upanissayai nava|

haitu uppādī dhammau nahaitussa nauppādissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava|

1-18. haituduka-atītattikaṃ

26. haitu atītau dhammau nahaitussa naatītassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava|

haitu  anāgatau dhammau nahaitussa naanāgatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava|

1-19. haituduka-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

27. haituṃ  atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naatītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-20. haituduka-ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ

28. haituṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (tīṇi)|

haituṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-21. haituduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

29. nahaitu sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nanahaitussa nasanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… nahaitu sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau nahaitussa nasanidassanasappaṭighassa ca nanahaitussa nasanidassanasappaṭighassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā upanissayai puraijātai atthiyā avigatai tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ|

haituṃ  anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca nahaituṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3) haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. sahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

30. sahaitukaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

ahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naahaitukau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

3-1. haitusampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

31. haitusampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau nakusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

haitusampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituvippayuttaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nahaituvippayuttau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

4-5-1. haitusahaitukādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

32. haitu caiva sahaitukau ca kusalau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa ca nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu caiva sahaitukau ca kusalau dhammau naahaitukassa caiva nana ca haitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu caiva sahaitukau  ca kusalau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa ca nakusalassa ca naahaitukassa caiva nana ca haitussa nakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu kusalau dhammau naahaitukassa caiva nana ca haitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa ca nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| sahaitukau  caiva na ca haitu kusalau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa ca nakusalassa ca naahaitukassa caiva nana ca haitussa nakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

haitu caiva sahaitukau kusalau ca sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu kusalau  ca dhammā nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu caiva sahaitukau kusalau ca sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu kusalau ca dhammā naahaitukassa caiva nana ca haitussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu caiva sahaitukau kusalau ca sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu kusalau ca dhammā nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa nakusalassa ca naahaitukassa caiva nana haitussa nakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

ārammaṇai nava|

33. haitu caiva sahaitukau ca akusalau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa naakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (aitaina upāyaina nava pañhā kātabbā|)

34. haitu caiva sahaitukau ca abyākatau dhammau nahaitussa caiva naahaitukassa ca naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (nava pañhā kātabbā|) (saṃkhittaṃ, haituhaitusampayuttadukaṃ haitusahaitukadukasadisaṃ| saṃkhittaṃ| nava pañhā|)

6-1. nahaitusahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

35. nahaituṃ  sahaitukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nahaitu naahaitukau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

haitugaucchakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7-8-1. sappac‍cayādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

36. sappac‍cayaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappac‍cayau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sappac‍cayaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappac‍cayau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sappac‍cayaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naappac‍cayau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… (saṃkhittaṃ sappac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

9-10-1. sanidassanādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

37. anidassanaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (akusalaṃ kusalasadisaṃ|)

anidassanaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

appaṭighaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

appaṭighaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappaṭighau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākatau aikaṃ|

11-1. rūpīduka-kusalattikaṃ

38. arūpiṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

arūpiṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

rūpiṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā narūpī naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

12-1. laukiyaduka-kusalattikaṃ

39. laukiyaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

laukiyaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

laukiyaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nalaukiyau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

13-1. kainaciviñ‍ñaiyyaduka-kusalattikaṃ

40. kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

cūḷantaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

14-1. āsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

41. nauāsavaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

āsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nauāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanauāsavau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

15-1. sāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

42. sāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

sāsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sāsavaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasāsavau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

16-1. āsavasampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

43. āsavavippayuttaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

āsavasampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

āsavavippayuttaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naāsavasampayuttau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … haituyā tīṇi|

17-18-1. āsavasāsavādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

44. sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

(āsavaāsavasampayuttadukaṃ āsavasāsavadukasadisaṃ|)

19-1. āsavavippayuttasāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

45. āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ  sāsavaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ  sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

āsavagaucchakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

20-54-1. sañ‍ñaujanādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

46. nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nausañ‍ñaujanau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nauganthaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nauaughaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauaughau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nauyaugaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauyaugau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… naunīvaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naunīvaraṇau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nauparāmāsaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauparāmāsau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

55-1. sārammaṇaduka-kusalattikaṃ

47. sārammaṇaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasārammaṇau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sārammaṇaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasārammaṇau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

anārammaṇaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanārammaṇau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

56-1. cittaduka-kusalattikaṃ

48. cittaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naucittaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanaucittau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

57-1. caitasikaduka-kusalattikaṃ

49. caitasikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacaitasikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

caitasikaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacaitasikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

acaitasikaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naacaitasikau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

58-1. cittasampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

50. cittasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

cittasampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

59-1. cittasaṃsaṭṭhaduka-kusalattikaṃ

51. cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

60-1. cittasamuṭṭhānaduka-kusalattikaṃ

52. cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

61-1. cittasahabhūduka-kusalattikaṃ

53. cittasahabhuṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasahabhū nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

cittasahabhuṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasahabhū naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

62-1. cittānuparivattiduka-kusalattikaṃ

54. cittānuparivattiṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittānuparivattī nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

cittānuparivattiṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittānuparivattī naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

63-1. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaduka-kusalattikaṃ
