55. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

64-1. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhūduka-kusalattikaṃ

56. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva pac‍cayavasaina|)

65-1. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiduka-kusalattikaṃ

57. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

66-1. ajjhattikaduka-kusalattikaṃ

58. ajjhattikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bāhiraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ajjhattikaṃ kusalañ‍ca bāhiraṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

ajjhattikaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bāhiraṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| ajjhattikaṃ akusalañ‍ca bāhiraṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

67-1. upādāduka-kusalattikaṃ

59. nauupādā kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauupādā nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nauupādā akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauupādā naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ aikaṃyaiva|)

68-1. upādin‍naduka-kusalattikaṃ

60. anupādin‍naṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nau nakusalau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

anupādin‍naṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ aikaṃyaiva|)

mahantaradukaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

69-82-1. dvigaucchakāni-kusalattikaṃ

61. nauupādānaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādānau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

upādānaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādānau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naukilaisaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

83-1. dassanainapahātabbaduka-kusalattikaṃ

62. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanadassanaina pahātabbau naabyākatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

84-1. bhāvanāyapahātabbaduka-kusalattikaṃ

63. nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanabhāvanāya pahātabbau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

85-1. dassanainapahātabbahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

64. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai| (abyākatavārai sabbattha pac‍cayavasaina gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

86-1. bhāvanāyapahātabbahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

65. nabhāvanāya  pahātabbahaitukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

87-1. savitak‍kaduka-kusalattikaṃ

66. savitak‍kaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

savitak‍kaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

88-1. savicāraduka-kusalattikaṃ

67. savicāraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavicārau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

savicāraṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavicārau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

89-1. sappītikaduka-kusalattikaṃ

68. sappītikaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| appītikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sappītikaṃ kusalañ‍ca appītikaṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

sappītikaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| appītikaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sappītikaṃ akusalañ‍ca appītikaṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

90-1. pītisahagataduka-kusalattikaṃ

69. pītisahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

pītisahagataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

91-1. sukhasahagataduka-kusalattikaṃ

70. sukhasahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhasahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhasahagataṃ kusalañ‍ca nasukhasahagataṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau nakusalau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

sukhasahagataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhasahagataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhasahagataṃ akusalañ‍ca nasukhasahagataṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

92-1. upaikkhāsahagataduka-kusalattikaṃ

71. upaikkhāsahagataṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naupaikkhāsahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upaikkhāsahagataṃ kusalañ‍ca naupaikkhāsahagataṃ kusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

upaikkhāsahagataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naupaikkhāsahagataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upaikkhāsahagataṃ akusalañ‍ca naupaikkhāsahagataṃ akusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi| (abyākatamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|)

93-1. kāmāvacaraduka-kusalattikaṃ

72. kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanakāmāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanakāmāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

kāmāvacaraṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanakāmāvacarau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

94-1. rūpāvacaraduka-kusalattikaṃ

73. rūpāvacaraṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| narūpāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

narūpāvacaraṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

95-1. arūpāvacaraduka-kusalattikaṃ

74. arūpāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naarūpāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

naarūpāvacaraṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

96-1. pariyāpan‍naduka-kusalattikaṃ

75. pariyāpan‍naṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapariyāpan‍nau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| apariyāpan‍naṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapariyāpan‍nau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

pariyāpan‍naṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapariyāpan‍nau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

97-1. niyyānikaduka-kusalattikaṃ

76. niyyānikaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyyānikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aniyyānikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyyānikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

aniyyānikaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyyānikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlaṃ dvai|)

98-1. niyataduka-kusalattikaṃ

77. niyataṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aniyataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

niyataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aniyataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naniyatau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai| (abyākatamūlai dvai|)

99-1. sauttaraduka-kusalattikaṃ

78. sauttaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanuttarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anuttaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanuttarau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

sauttaraṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanuttarau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (abyākatamūlai dvai|)

100-1. saraṇaduka-kusalattikaṃ

79. araṇaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

80. araṇau abyākatau dhammau naaraṇassa naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau abyākatau dhammau nasaraṇassa naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (2)

ārammaṇai  dvai…pai॰… avigatai dvai|

100-2. saraṇaduka-vaidanāttikaṃ

81. saraṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naaraṇau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

araṇaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

haituyā cattāri|

82. saraṇaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tīṇi|

saraṇaṃ  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…|

haituyā cattāri|

100-3. saraṇaduka-vipākattikaṃ

83. araṇaṃ vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-4. saraṇaduka-upādin‍nattikaṃ

84. araṇaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naupādin‍nupādāniyau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-5. saraṇaduka-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

85. saraṇaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-6. saraṇaduka-vitak‍kattikaṃ

86. saraṇaṃ  savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

araṇaṃ  savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

87. saraṇaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cattāri|

araṇaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-7. saraṇaduka-pītittikaṃ

88. saraṇaṃ  pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

araṇaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

araṇaṃ sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cattāri|

100-8. saraṇaduka-dassanattikaṃ

89. saraṇaṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-9. saraṇaduka-dassanahaituttikaṃ

90. saraṇaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

saraṇaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-10. saraṇaduka-ācayagāmittikaṃ

91. saraṇaṃ ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

100-11. saraṇaduka-saikkhattikaṃ

92. araṇaṃ saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ  asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ  naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-12. saraṇaduka-parittattikaṃ

93. araṇaṃ  parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-13. saraṇaduka-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

94. saraṇaṃ parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naparittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naparittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naappamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-14. saraṇaduka-hīnattikaṃ

95. saraṇaṃ hīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ  majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau namajjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau namajjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ paṇītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau napaṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-15. saraṇaduka-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

96. saraṇaṃ  micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-16. saraṇaduka-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

97. araṇaṃ maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namaggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namaggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau namaggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-17. saraṇaduka-uppan‍nattikaṃ

98. saraṇau anuppan‍nau dhammau nasaraṇassa naanuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cattāri|

araṇau  uppādī dhammau naaraṇassa nauppādissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau uppādī dhammau nasaraṇassa nauppādissa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇai dvai|

100-18. saraṇaduka-atītattikaṃ

99. saraṇau atītau dhammau nasaraṇassa naatītassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cattāri| (anāgatau atītasadisau|)

100-19. saraṇaduka-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

100. saraṇaṃ  atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naatītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naatītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-21. saraṇaduka-ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ

101. saraṇaṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ  bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-22. saraṇaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

102. araṇaṃ  anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (aikaṃ|)

saraṇaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca araṇaṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

103. araṇaṃ  anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| (1)

saraṇaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā|

nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

104. saraṇau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau nasaraṇassa naanidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

araṇau  anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau nasaraṇassa naanidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haituyā dvai, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

105. saraṇau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau nasaraṇassa naanidassanaappaṭighassa dhammassa sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, kammapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nauavigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ, aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukatikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-haitudukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

1. kusalaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (5)

abyākataṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu ca naakusalau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

pac‍canīyaṃ

naārammaṇapac‍cayau

2. kusalaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā tairasa…pai॰… navippayuttai nava|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. kusalau haitu dhammau nakusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau haitu dhammau naakusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa  haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau haitu dhammau naabyākatassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau haitu dhammau naakusalassa nahaitussa ca naabyākatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau haitu dhammau nakusalassa nahaitussa ca naakusalassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

akusalau haitu dhammau naakusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

abyākatau haitu dhammau nakusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

kusalau haitu dhammau nakusalassa nahaitussa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4. haituyā  tairasa, ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

5. kusalaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (sabbattha nava|)

1-2. kusalattika-sahaitukadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

6. kusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau ca naakusalau nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau ca naakusalau nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasahaitukau ca naakusalau nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

haituyā  nava, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai aikādasa| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

7. kusalau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau sahaitukau dhammau naakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa ca naakusalassa nasahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

akusalau  sahaitukau dhammau naakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| akusalau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa ca naakusalassa  nasahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau naakusalassa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau nakusalassa nasahaitukassa ca naakusalassa nasahaitukassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

8. haituyā nava, ārammaṇai pan‍narasa…pai॰… avigatai aikādasa|

9. akusalaṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau ca naabyākatau naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ  ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau ca naakusalau naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

haituyā cha, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai cha|

1-3. kusalattika-haitusampayuttadukaṃ

10. kusalaṃ  haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sahaitukadukasadisaṃ|)

1-4. kusalattika-haitusahaitukadukaṃ

11. kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva  sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naabyākatau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

akusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā nava|

12. kusalaṃ  sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu  ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca naabyākatau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca naabyākatau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ  sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca naakusalau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

1-5. kusalattika-haituhaitusampayuttadukaṃ

13. kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca naabyākatau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

(akusalaṃ tīṇi kātabbaṃ, abyākataṃ tīṇi  kātabbaṃ|)

haituyā nava|

14. kusalaṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaituvippayuttau caiva  nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nahaituvippayuttau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaituvippayuttau caiva nanahaitu ca naabyākatau nahaituvippayuttau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇi| abyākataṃ… tīṇi| haituyā nava|

1-6. kusalattika-haitusahaitukadukaṃ

15. kusalaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau ca naakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau…pai॰… naakusalau…pai॰… nakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau ca naakusalau nahaitu nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

16. abyākataṃ nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nahaitu naahaitukau ca naakusalau nahaitu naahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

haitugaucchakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

1-7-8. kusalattika-sappac‍cayādidukāni

17. abyākatau  appac‍cayau dhammau naabyākatassa naappac‍cayassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau appac‍cayau dhammau nakusalassa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau appac‍cayau dhammau naakusalassa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau appac‍cayau dhammau naakusalassa naappac‍cayassa ca naabyākatassa naappac‍cayassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau appac‍cayau dhammau nakusalassa naappac‍cayassa ca naakusalassa naappac‍cayassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (5)

ārammaṇai pañ‍ca| (asaṅkhataṃ appac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

1-9. kusalattika-sanidassanadukaṃ

18. abyākatau  sanidassanau dhammau naabyākatassa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| abyākatau sanidassanau dhammau nakusalassa nasanidassanassa…pai॰… (cha pañhā kātabbā)|

kusalaṃ anidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaina tīṇiyaiva| abyākataina tīṇiyaiva| haituyā nava|

1-10. kusalattika-sappaṭighadukaṃ

19. abyākataṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasappaṭighau ca naakusalau nasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ appaṭighaina tīṇi| akusalaṃ appaṭighaina tīṇi| abyākataṃ appaṭighaina tīṇi| kusalaṃ appaṭighañ‍ca abyākataṃ appaṭighañ‍ca tīṇi| akusalaṃ appaṭighañ‍ca abyākataṃ  appaṭighañ‍ca tīṇi| haituyā pan‍narasa|

1-11. kusalattika-rūpīdukaṃ

20. abyākataṃ  rūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ rūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ rūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau narūpī ca naakusalau narūpī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca tīṇi| akusalaṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca tīṇi| abyākataṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca tīṇi| haituyā nava|

1-12. kusalattika-laukiyadukaṃ

21. abyākataṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukiyaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naakusalau nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naakusalau nalaukiyau ca naabyākatau nalaukiyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau nalaukiyau ca naakusalau nalaukiyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca|

kusalaṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nalaukuttarau ca naakusalau nalaukuttarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nalaukuttarau ca naakusalau nalaukuttarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

1-13. kusalattika-kainaciviñ‍ñaiyyadukaṃ

22. kusalaṃ  kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nakainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā aikūnavīsati|

23. kusalaṃ kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(aitaina upāyaina kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyai aikūnavīsati pañhā kātabbā|)

1-14-19. kusalattika-āsavagaucchakaṃ

24. akusalaṃ  āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naāsavau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau ca naabyākatau naāsavau ca dhammā…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau ca naakusalau naāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nanauāsavau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau nanauāsavau ca naabyākatau nanauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

25. abyākataṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… (laukiyadukasadisaṃ)|

26. akusalaṃ  āsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naāsavasampayuttau dhammau …pai॰… nakusalau naāsavasampayuttau dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavasampayuttau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavasampayuttau ca naabyākatau naāsavasampayuttau ca dhammā…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavasampayuttau ca naakusalau naāsavasampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naāsavavippayuttau dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavavippayuttau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavavippayuttau ca naabyākatau naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

27. akusalaṃ āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca naabyākatau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammā…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau  caiva naanāsavau ca naakusalau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ  sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau naabyākatau naanāsavau caiva nanau āsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

28. akusalaṃ āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammau…pai॰… nakusalau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca naabyākatau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau…pai॰… naabyākatau naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau …pai॰… nakusalau naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanauāsavau ca naabyākatau naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

29. abyākataṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… (laukiyadukasadisaṃ)|

1-20-54. kusalattika-chagaucchakadukāni

30. akusalaṃ  sañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca…pai॰… ganthaṃ…pai॰… aughaṃ…pai॰… yaugaṃ…pai॰… nīvaraṇaṃ…pai॰… parāmāsaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

1-55. kusalattika-sārammaṇadukaṃ

31. kusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nakusalau nasārammaṇau ca naakusalau nasārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasārammaṇau ca naakusalau nasārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

haituyā nava|

32. abyākataṃ anārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanārammaṇau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanārammaṇau ca naakusalau naanārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi|

1-56. kusalattika-cittadukaṃ

33. kusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittau ca naabyākatau nacittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau ca naakusalau nacittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau ca naabyākatau nacittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ  cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau ca naakusalau nacittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittau ca naakusalau nacittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| haituyā tairasa|

34. kusalaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanaucittau ca naabyākatau nanaucittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ  naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittau ca naabyākatau nanaucittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākatāni tīṇi| haituyā nava|

1-57. kusalattika-caitasikadukaṃ

35. kusalaṃ  caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacaitasikau ca naabyākatau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau ca naakusalau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau ca naabyākatau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau ca naakusalau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau  nacaitasikau ca naakusalau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

haituyā tairasa| acaitasikāni nava|

1-58. kusalattika-cittasampayuttadukaṃ

36. kusalaṃ  cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau ca naabyākatau nacittasampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasampayuttau ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasampayuttau ca naakusalau nacittasampayuttau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa| cittavippayuttai tīṇi|

1-59. kusalattika-cittasaṃsaṭṭhadukaṃ

37. kusalaṃ  cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naabyākatau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naabyākatau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naakusalau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa|

1-60. kusalattika-cittasamuṭṭhānadukaṃ

38. kusalaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasamuṭṭhānau ca naabyākatau nacittasamuṭṭhānau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naabyākatau nacittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasamuṭṭhānau ca naabyākatau nacittasamuṭṭhānau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

39. kusalaṃ naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ  naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā tairasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

1-61. kusalattika-cittasahabhūdukaṃ

40. kusalaṃ cittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ cittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nacittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ cittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nacittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā tairasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

41. kusalaṃ naucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ naucittasahabhuṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanaucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ  naucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

1-62-65. kusalattika-cittānuparivattādidukāni

42. kusalaṃ cittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ naucittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa| (aitai saṃkhittā, dukattayaṃ cittadukasadisaṃ|)

1-66-68. kusalattika-ajjhattikādidukāni

43. kusalaṃ  ajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (saṃkhittaṃ, cittadukasadisaṃ)|

kusalaṃ bāhiraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nabāhirau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

44. abyākataṃ upādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ nauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… nava|

45. abyākataṃ  upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupādin‍nau ca naakusalau naupādin‍nau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

1-69-82. kusalattika-upādānādidukāni

46. akusalaṃ upādānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nauupādānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

akusalaṃ kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

1-83. kusalattika-piṭṭhidukaṃ

47. akusalaṃ  dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbau ca naakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau nanadassanaina pahātabbau ca naabyākatau nanadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

48. akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

49. akusalaṃ  dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

50. akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

51. kusalaṃ  savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

52. kusalaṃ savicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ avicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

53. kusalaṃ sappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tairasa|

kusalaṃ appītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

54. kusalaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanapītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

55. kusalaṃ sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ  nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

56. kusalaṃ  upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

57. abyākataṃ kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

58. kusalaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

abyākataṃ narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

59. kusalaṃ  arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

abyākataṃ naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanaarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

60. abyākataṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau napariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

kusalaṃ  apariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naapariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

61. kusalaṃ niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau naaniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

62. kusalaṃ  niyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

abyākataṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

63. abyākataṃ sauttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nasauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

kusalaṃ anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

64. akusalaṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasaraṇau ca naakusalau nasaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ araṇaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau naaraṇau ca naabyākatau naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. vaidanāttika-haitudukaṃ

65. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… satta pañhā|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta pañhā|

adukkhamasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta pañhā| haituyā aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa| (sabbattha aikavīsa|)

66. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā nava|

3-1. vipākattika-haitudukaṃ

67. vipākaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau  nahaitu ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nahaitu ca navipākadhammadhammau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi… haituyā tairasa|

68. vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nanahaitu ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…| naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākau nanahaitu ca navipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

vipākaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cuddasa|

4-1. upādin‍nattika-haitudukaṃ

69. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca… haituyā tairasa|

70. upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā nava|

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-haitudukaṃ

71. saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā nava|

6-1. vitak‍kattika-haitudukaṃ

72. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pan‍narasa|

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

7-1. pītittika-haitudukaṃ

73. pītisahagataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

pītisahagataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa haituyā aṭṭha?|

8-1. dassanattika-haitudukaṃ

74. dassanaina pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

9-1. dassanahaituttika-haitudukaṃ

75. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā sauḷasa|

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

10-1. ācayagāmittika-haitudukaṃ

76. ācayagāmiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naācayagāmī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

ācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naapacayagāmī nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

11-1. saikkhattika-haitudukaṃ

77. saikkhaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaikkhau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

saikkhaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaikkhau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

12-1. parittattika-haitudukaṃ

78. parittaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

parittaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cuddasa|

13-1. parittārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

79. parittārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naparittārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

parittārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namahaggatārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

14-1. hīnattika-haitudukaṃ

80. hīnaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahīnau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

hīnaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namajjhimau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

15-1. micchattaniyatattika-haitudukaṃ

81. micchattaniyataṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namicchattaniyatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

micchattaniyataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasammattaniyatau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

16-1. maggārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

82. maggārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namaggārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍cavīsa|

maggārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca namaggahaitukau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa haituyā aṭṭha?|

17-1. uppan‍nattika-haitudukaṃ

83. uppan‍nau  haitu dhammau naanupan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| uppan‍nau haitu dhammau nauppādissa  nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| uppan‍nau haitu dhammau naanuppan‍nassa nahaitussa ca nauppādissa nahaitussa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā tīṇi|

18-1. atītattika-haitudukaṃ

84. pac‍cuppan‍nau haitu dhammau naatītassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuppan‍nau haitu dhammau naanāgatassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuppan‍nau haitu dhammau naatītassa nahaitussa ca naanāgatassa nahaitussa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā tīṇi|

19-1. atītārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

85. atītārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naatītārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

atītārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāgatārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

20-1. ajjhattattika-haitudukaṃ

86. ajjhattaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhā nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bahiddhā haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

ajjhattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhā nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| bahiddhā nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

21-1. ajjhattārammaṇattika-haitudukaṃ

87. ajjhattārammaṇaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

ajjhattārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabahiddhārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

22-1. sanidassanattika-haitudukaṃ

88. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

89. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanahaitu ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-2. sanidassanattika-sahaitukadukaṃ

90. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasahaitukau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi|

22-3. sanidassanattika-haitusampayuttadukaṃ

91. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitusampayuttau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitusampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-4. sanidassanattika-haitusahaitukadukaṃ

92. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-5. sanidassanattika-haituhaitusampayuttadukaṃ

93. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaituvippayuttau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-6. sanidassanattika-nahaitusahaitukadukaṃ

94. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-7. sanidassanattika-sappac‍cayadukaṃ

95. anidassanaappaṭighau appac‍cayau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, upanissayai tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

22-9. sanidassanattika-sanidassanadukaṃ

96. sanidassanasappaṭighau nasanidassanau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā tīṇi, upanissayai puraijātai atthiyā avigatai tīṇi|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ anidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

22-10. sanidassanattika-sappaṭighadukaṃ

97. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ sappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasappaṭighau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  appaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ appaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ appaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naappaṭighau ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

22-11. sanidassanattika-rūpīdukaṃ

98. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  rūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naarūpī ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naarūpī ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-12. sanidassanattika-laukiyadukaṃ

99. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ)| haituyā tīṇi… avigatai tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-13. sanidassanattika-kainaciviñ‍ñaiyyadukaṃ

100. anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyañ‍ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… cha… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

22-14. sanidassanattika-āsavadukaṃ

101. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nauāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanauāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanauāsavau  ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-15. sanidassanattika-sāsavadukaṃ

102. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanidassanasappaṭighau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-16. sanidassanattika-āsavasampayuttadukaṃ

103. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  āsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  naāsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavasampayuttau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavasampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-17. sanidassanattika-āsavasāsavadukaṃ

104. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau caiva nanauāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanasappaṭighaṃ sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau naanidassanasappaṭighau naanāsavau caiva nanau āsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-18. sanidassanattika-āsavaāsavasampayuttadukaṃ

105. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavau  caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-19. sanidassanattika-āsavavippayuttasāsavadukaṃ

106. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nasanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-20-54. sanidassanattika-sañ‍ñaujanādidukāni

107. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  sañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nausañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

108. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ ganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|
