anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauaughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ yaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauyaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

109. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  nīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naunīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

110. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ parāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauparāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

22-55. sanidassanattika-sārammaṇadukaṃ

111. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasārammaṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naanārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-56. sanidassanattika-cittadukaṃ

112. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naucittau  ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naucittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-57. sanidassanattika-caitasikadukaṃ

113. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacaitasikau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nacaitasikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-58. sanidassanattika-cittasampayuttadukaṃ

114. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  …pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasampayuttau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-59. sanidassanattika-cittasaṃsaṭṭhadukaṃ

115. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-60. sanidassanattika-cittasamuṭṭhānadukaṃ

116. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-61. sanidassanattika-cittasahabhūdukaṃ

117. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  cittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-62. sanidassanattika-cittānuparivattidukaṃ

118. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-63-65. sanidassanattika-cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānādidukāni

119. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

120. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … haituyā cha|

121. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-66. sanidassanattika-ajjhattadukaṃ

122. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ ajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naajjhattikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ ajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naajjhattikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ ajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naajjhattikau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naajjhattikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha| ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai cha|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ  bāhiraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabāhirau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  aikādasa|

22-67. sanidassanattika-upādādukaṃ

123. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nanauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha|

anidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauupādā ca anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nauupādā ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

22-68. sanidassanattika-upādin‍nadukaṃ

124. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naupādin‍nau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naupādin‍nau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

125. sanidassanasappaṭighau anupādin‍nau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa naanupādin‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai nava|

22-69-82. sanidassanattika-upādānādidukāni

126. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  upādānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nauupādānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

127. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naukilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-83. sanidassanattika-dassanainapahātabbadukaṃ

128. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau  nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanidassanasappaṭighau nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanadassanaina pahātabbau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanadassanaina pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-84. sanidassanattika-bhāvanāyapahātabbadukaṃ

129. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-85. sanidassanattika-dassanainapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ

130. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-86. sanidassanattika-bhāvanāyapahātabbahaitukadukaṃ

131. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-87. sanidassanattika-savitak‍kadukaṃ

132. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  nasavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ savitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasavitak‍kau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasavitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

133. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naavitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naavitak‍kau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naavitak‍kau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-88. sanidassanattika-savicāradukaṃ

134. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  savicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  avicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naavicārau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naavicārau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

22-89. sanidassanattika-sappītikadukaṃ

135. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  sappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasappītikau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nasappītikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ appītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ appītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ appītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naappītikau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naappītikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  tīṇi|

22-90. sanidassanattika-pītisahagatadukaṃ

136. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau napītisahagatau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau napītisahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanapītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-91. sanidassanattika-sukhasahagatadukaṃ

137. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau  nanasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-92. sanidassanattika-upaikkhāsahagatadukaṃ

138. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naupaikkhāsahagatau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naupaikkhāsahagatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanaupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-93. sanidassanattika-kāmāvacaradukaṃ

139. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  kāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nanakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanakāmāvacarau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nanakāmāvacarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-94. sanidassanattika-rūpāvacaradukaṃ

140. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-95. sanidassanattika-arūpāvacaradukaṃ

141. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ arūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanaarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-96. sanidassanattika-pariyāpan‍nadukaṃ

142. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau napariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  apariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naapariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ apariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naapariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ apariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naapariyāpan‍nau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naapariyāpan‍nau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-97. sanidassanattika-niyyānikadukaṃ

143. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ niyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-98. sanidassanattika-niyatadukaṃ

144. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ niyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-99. sanidassanattika-sauttaradukaṃ

145. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sauttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-100. sanidassanattika-saraṇadukaṃ

146. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

147. sanidassanasappaṭighau araṇau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa naaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

1. kusalaṃ  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… kusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

akusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… akusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā tairasa, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai tairasa|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitunaārammaṇapac‍cayādi

2. abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā … tīṇi|

3. kusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| kusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naārammaṇapac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| abyākataṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti naārammaṇapac‍cayā| tīṇi|

4. nahaituyā tīṇi, naārammaṇai nava, naadhipatiyā tairasa…pai॰… nauvigatai nava|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai nava|

nahaitupac‍cayā  ārammaṇai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi  pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

5. kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau nakusalassa nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…|

ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa| (pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

akusalapadaṃ

haitupac‍cayau

6. akusalaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… akusalaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai aṭṭha|

abyākatapadaṃ

pac‍canīyaṃ

7. abyākataṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nadukkhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau ca naakusalau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti nahaitupac‍cayā|

nahaituyā tīṇi| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

8. kusalaṃ  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tairasa| (sabbattha vitthārau)|

1-2. kusalattika-vipākattikaṃ

9. abyākataṃ  vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

10. kusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau navipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) …haituyā cha|

11. abyākataṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca naakusalau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

1-3. kusalattika-upādin‍nattikaṃ

12. abyākataṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau  naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca naakusalau naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

13. kusalau  anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nakusalassa naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau  dhammau naakusalassa naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| kusalau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nakusalassa naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca naakusalassa naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

akusalai tīṇi| abyākataṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyai tīṇiyaiva…pai॰…|

14. kusalaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| …haituyā cha|

1-4. kusalattika-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

15. akusalaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau  naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naabyākatau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  ca naabyākatau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca naakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā cha|

16. kusalaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cha|

1-5. kusalattika-vitak‍kattikaṃ

17. kusalaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa| (kusalai pañ‍ca, akusalai pañ‍ca, abyākatai tīṇi|)

kusalaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tairasa|

kusalaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

abyākataṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cha|

1-6. kusalattika-pītittikaṃ

18. kusalaṃ pītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ  sukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca… haituyā tairasa|

kusalaṃ upaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca… haituyā tairasa|

1-7. kusalattika-dassanattikaṃ

19. akusalaṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-8. kusalattika-dassanahaituttikaṃ

20. akusalaṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

akusalaṃ naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-9. kusalattika-ācayagāmittikaṃ

21. kusalaṃ ācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

kusalaṃ apacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naapacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ naivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-10. kusalattika-saikkhattikaṃ

22. kusalaṃ saikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (dvai mūlāni|)… haituyā cha|

abyākataṃ  asaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naasaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ naivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-11. kusalattika-parittattikaṃ

23. abyākataṃ parittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naparittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ  mahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau namahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

kusalaṃ appamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naappamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-12. kusalattika-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

24. kusalaṃ parittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naparittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kusalaṃ mahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau namahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kusalaṃ  appamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naappamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-13. kusalattika-hīnattikaṃ

25. akusalaṃ hīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau nahīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ majjhimaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau namajjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

kusalaṃ paṇītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau napaṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-14. kusalattika-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

26. akusalaṃ  micchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau namicchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ  sammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nasammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

abyākataṃ  aniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naabyākatau naaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-15. kusalattika-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

27. kusalaṃ maggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau namaggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

kusalaṃ maggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau namaggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

kusalaṃ maggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau namaggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-16. kusalattika-uppan‍nattikaṃ

28. kusalau anuppan‍nau dhammau nakusalassa naanuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa| (uppādī anuppan‍nasadisaṃ|)

1-17. kusalattika-atītattikaṃ

29. kusalau  atītau dhammau nakusalassa naatītassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa| (anāgataṃ atītasadisaṃ|)

1-18. kusalattika-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

30. kusalaṃ atītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naatītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kusalaṃ  anāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kusalaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-19-20. kusalattika-ajjhattattikadvayaṃ

31. kusalau ajjhattau dhammau naajjhattassa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)… ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa|

kusalau bahiddhā dhammau nabahiddhā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)… ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa|

32. kusalaṃ ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  nava|

kusalaṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-21. kusalattika-sanidassanattikaṃ

33. abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau naabyākatassa nasanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha| (tīṇi vaiditakaṃ kātabbaṃ|)

34. abyākataṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  nakusalau naanidassanasappaṭighau ca naakusalau naanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

35. kusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| kusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau  naanidassanaappaṭighau ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

akusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

abyākataṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| abyākataṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau ca naakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

kusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca abyākataṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

akusalaṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca abyākataṃ anidassanaappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakusalau naanidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā pan‍narasa|

2-1. vaidanāttika-kusalattikaṃ

36. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| satta|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta… haituyā  cuddasa|

37. sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| (aikavīsati pañhā|)

38. sukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau abyākatau dhammau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa naabyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

ārammaṇai aikavīsati| (dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaabyākatamūlaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaabyākatamūlampi kātabbaṃ|)

3-1. vipākattika-kusalattikaṃ

39. vipākadhammadhammaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

vipākadhammadhammaṃ  akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca navipākadhammadhammau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

4-1. upādin‍nattika-kusalattikaṃ

40. anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi haituyā cha|

anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nupādāniyau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naupādin‍nupādāniyau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-kusalattikaṃ

41. asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cha|

saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cha…pai॰… avigatai cha|

6-1. vitak‍kattika-kusalattikaṃ

42. savitak‍kasavicāraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naavitak‍kavicāramattau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pan‍narasa|

savitak‍kasavicāraṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasavitak‍kasavicārau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

7-1. pītittika-kusalattikaṃ

43. pītisahagataṃ  kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

sukhasahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

upaikkhāsahagataṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| (vaidanāttikasadisaṃ| saṃkhittaṃ|)

22-1. sanidassanattika-kusalattikaṃ

44. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nakusalau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naakusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cha…pai॰… avigatai cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau naabyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naanidassanasappaṭighau naabyākatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau  naabyākatau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naabyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

22-2. sanidassanattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

45. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-3. sanidassanattika-vipākattikaṃ

46. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  navipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau navipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-4. sanidassanattika-upādin‍nattikaṃ

47. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

sanidassanasappaṭighau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nasanidassanasappaṭighassa naanupādin‍nupādāniyassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (saṃkhittaṃ|)  ārammaṇai nava, anantarai tīṇi…pai॰… upanissayai puraijātai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ anupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naanupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-5. sanidassanattika-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

48. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasanidassanasappaṭighau naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-6-20. sanidassanattika-vitak‍kattikādi

49. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nasavitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau  naavitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau naavitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-21. sanidassanattika-ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ

50. anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  ajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau naajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

anidassanaappaṭighaṃ  bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anidassanaappaṭighaṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰… anidassanaappaṭighaṃ bahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  cha…pai॰… avigatai cha| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. haituduka-sahaitukadukaṃ

1. haituṃ  sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca nahaituṃ sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi, ārammaṇai aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca|

2. nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ|) nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. haitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

haitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau nanahaitussa nasahaitukassa  dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haitu sahaitukau dhammau  nahaitussa nasahaitukassa ca nanahaitussa nasahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau nanahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa nasahaitukassa ca nanahaitussa nasahaitukassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|)

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

4. haituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cattāri|

1-2. haituduka-haitusampayuttadukaṃ

5. haituṃ haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (sahaitukasadisaṃ|)

haituṃ haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nahaituṃ haituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nahaituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā cattāri|

1-3-5. haituduka-haitusahaitukādidukāni

6. haituṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

7. haituṃ  haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nahaituvippayuttau caiva nanahaitu ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

8. nahaituṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu nasahaitukau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā adhipatiyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

1-6-12. haituduka-cūḷantaradukādi

9. nahaitu appac‍cayau dhammau nanahaitussa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰… nahaitu appac‍cayau dhammau nahaitussa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu appac‍cayau dhammau nahaitussa naappac‍cayassa ca nanahaitussa naappac‍cayassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau…pai॰…| (asaṅkhataṃ appac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

10. nahaitu sanidassanau dhammau nanahaitussa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sanidassanau dhammau nahaitussa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu sanidassanau dhammau nahaitussa nasanidassanassa ca nanahaitussa nasanidassanassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇai tīṇi|

haituṃ anidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanidassanau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

11. nahaituṃ sappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikaṃ|

haituṃ  appaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

12. nahaituṃ  rūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu narūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ arūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naarūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

13. nahaituṃ laukiyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nalaukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ  laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ laukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ laukuttarañ‍ca nahaituṃ laukuttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nalaukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

14. haituṃ kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

haituṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-13-18. haituduka-āsavagaucchakaṃ

15. haituṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

haituṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

16. nahaituṃ  sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

haituṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

17. haituṃ āsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naāsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

haituṃ  āsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naāsavavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

18. haituṃ āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

haituṃ sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naanāsavau caiva nanau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

19. haituṃ  āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanauāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

20. nahaituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (laukiyasadisaṃ|)

haituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavañ‍ca nahaituṃ  āsavavippayuttaṃ anāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

1-19-53. haituduka-sañ‍ñaujanādigaucchakaṃ

21. haituṃ  sañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nausañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22. haituṃ ganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

23. haituṃ  aughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauaughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

24. haituṃ yaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauyaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

25. haituṃ nīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naunīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

26. nahaituṃ parāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nauparāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-54-81. haituduka-mahantaradukādi

27. haituṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ anārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naanārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

28. nahaituṃ cittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

haituṃ  naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nanaucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

29. haituṃ  caitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ acaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu naacaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

30. haituṃ cittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nacittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ cittavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nacittavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

31. haituṃ  cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ cittavisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nacittavisaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

32. haituṃ  cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

33. haituṃ cittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

34. haituṃ  cittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naucittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

35. haituṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

36. haituṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

37. haituṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi (saṃkhittaṃ| nayavasaina vitthāraitabbaṃ)|

1-82-98. haituduka-dassanainapahātabbadukādi

38. haituṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇimaiva|

39. haituṃ dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanahaitu nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikaṃ|

1-99. haituduka-saraṇadukaṃ

40. haituṃ  saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituṃ saraṇañ‍ca nahaituṃ saraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nasaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nanahaitu naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nahaitu naaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nahaitu naaraṇau ca nanahaitu naaraṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. sahaitukaduka-haitudukaṃ

41. sahaitukaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

ahaitukaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cha|

sahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau nanahaitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

3-1. haitusampayuttaduka-haitudukaṃ

42. haitusampayuttaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitusampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

haituvippayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cha|

haitusampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

4-1. haitusahaitukaduka-haitudukaṃ

43. haituñ‍caiva sahaitukaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naahaitukau caiva nana ca haitu nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

5-1. haituhaitusampayuttaduka-haitudukaṃ

44. haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu caiva  nahaituvippayuttau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaituvippayuttau caiva nana ca haitu nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

7-13-1. cūḷantaradukādi-haitudukaṃ

45. sappac‍cayaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappac‍cayau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

sappac‍cayaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappac‍cayau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (saṅkhataṃ sappac‍cayasadisaṃ)|

46. anidassanaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanidassanau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

anidassanaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasanidassanau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

47. appaṭighaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

appaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappaṭighau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

48. arūpiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

rūpiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpī nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

arūpiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpī nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

49. laukiyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

laukuttaraṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cattāri|

laukiyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukuttarau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

laukuttaraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nalaukiyau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

50. kainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

kainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ haituñ‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

14-1. āsavaduka-haitudukaṃ

51. āsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nauāsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavaṃ  haituñ‍ca nauāsavaṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ (sabbattha pañ‍ca|)

nauāsavaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauāsavau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

15-1. sāsavaduka-haitudukaṃ

52. sāsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

anāsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cattāri| (laukiyadukasadisaṃ|)

16-1. āsavasampayuttaduka-haitudukaṃ

53. āsavasampayuttaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavavippayuttaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavavippayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavasampayuttaṃ haituñ‍ca āsavavippayuttaṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā nava|

āsavasampayuttaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavavippayuttaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavasampayuttau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā cattāri|

17-1. āsavasāsavaduka-haitudukaṃ

54. āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… aikaṃ|

āsavañ‍caiva sāsavaṃ haituñ‍ca sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ haituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau caiva naanāsavau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā pañ‍ca|

āsavañ‍caiva sāsavañ‍ca nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanāsavau caiva nanauāsavau ca nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

18-1. āsavaāsavasampayuttaduka-haitudukaṃ

55. āsavañ‍caiva  āsavasampayuttañ‍ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau  caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavau caiva naāsavavippayuttau ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā cattāri|

āsavañ‍caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naāsavavippayuttau caiva nanau ca āsavau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cattāri|

19-1. āsavavippayuttasāsavaduka-haitudukaṃ

56. āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ  anāsavaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi… haituyā cattāri|

āsavavippayuttaṃ sāsavaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau naanāsavau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ  anāsavaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau nasāsavau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

20-54-1. sañ‍ñaujanagaucchakādi-haitudukaṃ

57. sañ‍ñaujanaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nausañ‍ñaujanau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

58. ganthaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

59. aughaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauaughau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

60. yaugaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauyaugau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

61. nīvaraṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naunīvaraṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

62. nauparāmāsaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauparāmāsau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

55-68-1. mahantaradukādi-haitudukaṃ

63. sārammaṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

sārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| anārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| sārammaṇaṃ  nahaituñ‍ca anārammaṇaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanārammaṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

64. naucittaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanaucittau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

cittaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

65. caitasikaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacaitasikau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

caitasikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naacaitasikau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

66. cittasampayuttaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

67. cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacittasaṃsaṭṭhau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

68. cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasamuṭṭhānau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

69. cittasahabhuṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasahabhū nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

70. cittānuparivattiṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittānuparivattī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

71. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

72. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

73. cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

74. bāhiraṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabāhirau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

ajjhattikaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naajjhattikau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

75. nauupādā  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanauupādā nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

upādā  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādā nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

76. upādin‍naṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

anupādin‍naṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā cattāri|

upādin‍naṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naanupādin‍nau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

anupādin‍naṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupādin‍nau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

69-82-1. upādānagaucchakādi-haitudukaṃ

77. upādānaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādānau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

78. kilaisaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naukilaisau  nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

83-1. piṭṭhiduka-haitudukaṃ

79. dassanaina  pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā cattāri|

dassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanadassanaina pahātabbau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nadassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

80. bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā cattāri|

bhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanabhāvanāya pahātabbau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nabhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

81. dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (sabbattha pañhai saṃkhittaṃ|)

dassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-1-6. saraṇaduka-haitudukādi

82. saraṇaṃ  haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

araṇaṃ haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā  cattāri|

saraṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nanahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

83. saraṇaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

84. saraṇaṃ haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| araṇaṃ haitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai| (sahaitukadukasadisaṃ|)

85. saraṇaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nahaitu  caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ haituñ‍caiva sahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

saraṇaṃ sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau naahaitukau caiva nana ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ  sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naahaitukau caiva nana ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

86. saraṇaṃ  haituñ‍caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nahaitu caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (haitusahaitukadukasadisaṃ|)

87. saraṇaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ nahaituṃ sahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu nasahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā dvai|

araṇaṃ  nahaituṃ ahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nahaitu naahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-7-13. saraṇaduka-cūḷantaradukādi

88. araṇau appac‍cayau dhammau nasaraṇassa naappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

89. araṇau asaṅkhatau dhammau nasaraṇassa naasaṅkhatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

90. araṇau sanidassanau dhammau naaraṇassa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| araṇau sanidassanau dhammau nasaraṇassa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-14-54. saraṇaduka-āsavādigaucchakāni

91. saraṇaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| saraṇaṃ āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nauāsavau ca naaraṇau nauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā|

saraṇaṃ  nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naaraṇau nanauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

92. araṇaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau nasāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

araṇaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (aitaina upāyaina sabbattha vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

100-55-82. saraṇaduka-mahantaradukādi

93. saraṇaṃ  sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| araṇaṃ sārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nasārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-83. saraṇaduka-piṭṭhidukaṃ

94. saraṇaṃ dassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

araṇaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naaraṇau nanadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

95. araṇaṃ sauttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nasaraṇau nasauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

araṇaṃ anuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasaraṇau naanuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammānulaumapac‍canīyai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

anulaumapac‍canīyapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1. kusalattikaṃ

1-2. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

1. nakusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| akusalaṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā…pai॰… dvai khandhai paṭic‍ca dvai khandhā nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ aikaṃ khandhaṃ paṭic‍ca tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañ‍ca rūpaṃ…pai॰… nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

nakusalañ‍ca  naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

2. nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| dvai|

naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| dvai|

naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| dvai|

nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalañ‍ca  naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati ārammaṇapac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā aṭṭhārasa, ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā aṭṭhārasa…pai॰… avigatai aṭṭhārasa|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

3. nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā…pai॰… nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā|

4. nakusalaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā…pai॰… nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

5. nahaituyā cha, naārammaṇai cha, naadhipatiyā aṭṭhārasa…pai॰… nauvigatai cha|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai cha… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai cha… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

(sahajātavārau paṭic‍cavārasadisau)|

3-6. pac‍cayavārādi

6. nakusalaṃ  dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau ca abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| pañ‍ca|

naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naakusalañ‍ca naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā… tīṇi| nakusalañ‍ca naakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā chabbīsati, ārammaṇai tairasa…pai॰… avigatai chabbīsati|

saṃsaṭṭhavārai haituyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

7. nakusalau  dhammau akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau dhammau akusalassa ca abyākatassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naakusalau dhammau kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naabyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pañ‍ca|

nakusalau ca naabyākatau  ca dhammā akusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nakusalau ca naakusalau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ|

ārammaṇapac‍cayau

8. nakusalau  dhammau kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naakusalau dhammau akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

naabyākatau dhammau abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naakusalau ca naabyākatau ca dhammā abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

nakusalau  ca naakusalau ca dhammā kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

9. haituyā aṭṭhārasa, ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa, adhipatiyā taivīsa…pai॰… avigatai dvāvīsa| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. vaidanāttikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

10. nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3)

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā aikavīsa, adhipatiyā aikavīsa…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa|

3. vipākattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

11. navipākaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca| haituyā dvāvīsa|

4. upādin‍nattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

12. naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naanupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naanupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca naanupādin‍naanupādāniyañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca naanupādin‍nupādāniyañ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

5. saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

13. nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

6. vitak‍kattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

14. nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|
