naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| haituyā aikūnapañ‍ñāsa|

7. pītittikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

15. napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cattāri|

nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cattāri|

naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cattāri| haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

8. dassanattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

16. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nabhāvanāya  pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina pahātabbau ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dassanaina  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

9. dassanahaituttikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

20. naparittaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvāvīsa|

23. namicchattaniyataṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

24. namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dasa haituyā aṭṭha?|

17. uppan‍nattikaṃ

7. pañhāvārau

25. naanuppan‍nau dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nauppādī dhammau uppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanuppan‍nau ca nauppādī ca dhammā uppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā tīṇi|

18-19. atītattikadvayaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

26. naatītau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanāgatau dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naatītau ca naanāgatau ca dhammā pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā  tīṇi|

27. naatītārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā sattarasa|

20-21. ajjhattattikadvayaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

28. naajjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nabahiddhā  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā dvai|

29. naajjhattārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai|

nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai|

naajjhattārammaṇañ‍ca nabahiddhārammaṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai| haituyā cha|

22. sanidassanattikaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

30. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanasappaṭighau ca anidassanaappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| satta|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| haituyā pañ‍catiṃsa| (pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai dukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-6. haitugaucchakaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

1. nahaituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ca nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ca nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nahaituñ‍ca nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍ca nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituñ‍ca nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ca nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti  haitupac‍cayā| (3) (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā nava, ārammaṇai nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

2. nasahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau ca ahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau ca ahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukañ‍ca naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau ca ahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| haituyā nava|

3. nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  nava|

4. nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

5. nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

6. nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

7-13. cūḷantaradukādi

7. naappac‍cayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

8. naasaṅkhataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saṅkhatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

9. nasanidassanaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau ca anidassanau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanau  dhammau sanidassanassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nasanidassanau dhammau anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanidassanau dhammau anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

10. nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau ca appaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau ca appaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nasappaṭighañ‍ca naappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā nava|

11. narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

12. nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau ca laukuttarau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

nalaukiyañ‍ca  nalaukuttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)| haituyā pañ‍ca|

13. nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

14-19. āsavagaucchakaṃ

14. naāsavaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naāsavaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau ca nauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau ca nauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naāsavañ‍ca nanauāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā nava|

15. nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau ca anāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nasāsavañ‍ca naanāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā pañ‍ca|

16. naāsavasampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

17. naāsavañ‍caiva anāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva sāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

18. naāsavañ‍caiva naāsavavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

19. āsavavippayuttaṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

20-54. sañ‍ñaujanādichagaucchakāni

20. nasañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|

naganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|

naaughaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā  …pai॰…|

nayaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca yaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|

nanīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…

21. naparāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…|

55-68. mahantaradukādi

22. nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau ca anārammaṇau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naanārammaṇaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nasārammaṇañ‍ca naanārammaṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā nava|

23. nacittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nacittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nacittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittau ca naucittau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

100. saraṇadukaṃ

1-7. paṭic‍cavārādi

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitupac‍cayau

(sahajātavārampi…pai॰… sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

57. nasaraṇau  dhammau araṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (1)

naaraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naaraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naaraṇau dhammau saraṇassa ca araṇassa ca dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

58. nasaraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasaraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai|

naaraṇau dhammau araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naaraṇau dhammau saraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| dvai|

59. haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai satta|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

1-1. haituduka-kusalattikaṃ

1. nahaituṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nahaitu kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu kusalau ca nahaitu kusalau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

2. nahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  tīṇi|

2-4-1. sahaitukādidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

3. nasahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sahaitukau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

4. nasahaitukaṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ naabyākatañ‍ca naahaitukaṃ naabyākatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

5. nahaitusampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā…|

6. nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca nakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva sahaitukassa ca kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca nakusalau dhammau sahaitukassa caiva na ca haitussa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca nakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva sahaitukassa kusalassa ca sahaitukassa caiva na ca haitussa kusalassa ca dhammassa  ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| tīṇi|

naahaitukau caiva nana ca haitu nakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva sahaitukassa ca kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu caiva naahaitukau nakusalau ca naahaitukau caiva nanahaitu nakusalau ca dhammā haitussa caiva sahaitukassa ca kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (sabbattha nava pañhā|)

7. nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva sahaitukassa ca akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ, nava pañhā)|

nahaitu caiva naahaitukau ca naabyākatau dhammau haitussa caiva sahaitukassa ca abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (nava pañhā|)

5-1. haituhaitusampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

8. nahaitu  caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca nakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva haitusampayuttassa ca kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ, nava pañhā kātabbā)|

nahaitu  caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca naakusalau dhammau haitussa caiva haitusampayuttassa ca akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau (saṃkhittaṃ, nava pañhā kātabbā)|

nahaitu  caiva nahaituvippayuttau ca naabyākatau dhammau haitussa caiva haitusampayuttassa ca abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (nava pañhā kātabbā|)

6-1. nahaitusahaitukaduka-kusalattikaṃ

7-13-1. cūḷantaradukāni-kusalattikaṃ

10. naappac‍cayaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sappac‍cayau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naappac‍cayaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sappac‍cayau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naappac‍cayaṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

11. naasaṅkhataṃ…pai॰… (sappac‍cayadukasadisaṃ)|

12. nasanidassanaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasanidassanaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasanidassanaṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau abyākatau ca anidassanau abyākatau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3) …haituyā tīṇi|

13. nasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā appaṭighau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

14. naarūpiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpī kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

naarūpiṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā arūpī akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

narūpiṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpī abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

15. nalaukuttaraṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā laukiyau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nalaukuttaraṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā laukuttarau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nalaukuttaraṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā laukiyau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nalaukiyaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nalaukuttaraṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

16. nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau kusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nakainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nakusalañ‍ca nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (sabbattha nava|)

nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

14-1. āsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

naāsavaṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanauāsavaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (dukamūlakaṃ aikaṃ|)… haituyā tīṇi|

15-1. sāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

18. naanāsavaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanāsavaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

naanāsavaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sāsavau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

16-1. āsavasampayuttaduka-kusalattikaṃ

19. naāsavasampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

17-1. āsavasāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

20. naāsavañ‍caiva  naanāsavañ‍ca nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sāsavau caiva nauāsavau ca kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavau caiva sāsavau ca akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

(āsavaāsavasampayuttadukaṃ natthi|)

19-1. āsavavippayuttasāsavaduka-kusalattikaṃ

21. āsavavippayuttaṃ  naanāsavaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau anāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau sāsavau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā āsavavippayuttau sāsavau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ nasāsavaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

83-100-1. piṭṭhidukāni-kusalattikaṃ

44. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā nadassanaina pahātabbau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  dvai|

57. naapariyāpan‍naṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā apariyāpan‍nau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naapariyāpan‍naṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā pariyāpan‍nau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nasaraṇaṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

1-2. haituduka-vaidanāttikaṃ

62. nahaituṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca nahaitu sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi| nahaitu nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttamūlaṃ tīṇiyaiva|

nahaituṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-3. haituduka-vipākattikaṃ

63. nahaituṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  navipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-4. haituduka-upādin‍nattikaṃ

64. nahaitu  naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nahaitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa  ca nahaitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (3)

nanahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau nahaitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi|

nahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca nanahaitu naupādin‍nupādāniyau ca dhammā haitussa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… tīṇi| (nava pañhā saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaituṃ naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naanupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-5. haituduka-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

65. nahaituṃ nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-6. haituduka-vitak‍kattikaṃ

66. nahaituṃ  nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-7. haituduka-pītittikaṃ

67. nahaituṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sukhasahagatau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-8. haituduka-dassanattikaṃ

68. nahaituṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

69. nahaituṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (dukamūlaṃ aikaṃ saṃkhittaṃ, sabbattha tīṇi|)

1-9. haituduka-dassanahaituttikaṃ

70. nanahaituṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dassanaina  pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

71. nahaituṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

1-10. haituduka-ācayagāmittikaṃ

72. nahaituṃ naācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naapacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

1-11. haituduka-saikkhattikaṃ

73. nahaituṃ  nasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu saikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu asaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

1-12. haituduka-parittattikaṃ

74. nahaituṃ  naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

nahaituṃ namahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naappamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu appamāṇau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-13. haituduka-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

75. nahaituṃ naparittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ namahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naappamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-14. haituduka-hīnattikaṃ

76. nahaituṃ nahīnaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu hīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ namajjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ namajjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

nahaituṃ napaṇītaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu paṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati  haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-15. haituduka-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

77. nahaituṃ namicchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nasammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

1-16. haituduka-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

78. nahaituṃ namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ namaggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  namaggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu maggādhipati dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-17. haituduka-uppan‍nattikaṃ

79. nahaitu nauppan‍nau dhammau haitussa uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava|

1-18. haituduka-atītattikaṃ

80. nahaitu napac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau haitussa pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai nava|

1-19. haituduka-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

81. nahaituṃ naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-20. haituduka-ajjhattattikaṃ

82. nahaitu  naajjhattau dhammau haitussa ajjhattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)… ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā upanissayai nava, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai tīṇi|

nahaitu  nabahiddhā dhammau haitussa bahiddhā dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)… ārammaṇai nava, adhipatiyā upanissayai nava, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai tīṇi|

(ajjhattattikau  na labbhati paṭic‍cavārādīsu|)

1-21. haituduka-ajjhattārammaṇattikaṃ

83. nahaituṃ naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇaṃ natthi|)

1-22. haituduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

84. nahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

(nahaitunaanidassanasappaṭighamūlaipi tīṇiyaiva|)

85. nahaituṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

2-5-22. sahaitukādidukāni-sanidassanattikaṃ

86. nasahaitukaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naahaitukaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi pañhā|

(nasahaitukanaanidassanasappaṭighamūlaipi tīṇiyaiva|)

nasahaitukaṃ  naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

(nahaitusampayuttaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ sahaitukadukasadisaṃ| tīṇi pañhā| haitusahaitukadukai ca haituhaitusampayuttadukai ca pañhā nau labbhati|)

6-22. nahaitusahaitukaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

87. nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nasahaitukaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇiyaiva|

nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

7-8-22. sappac‍cayadukādi-sanidassanattikaṃ

88. naappac‍cayaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naappac‍cayaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

(naappac‍cayanaanidassanaappaṭighamūlaipi aikamaiva|) haituyā aikaṃ, adhipatiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ|

(nasaṅkhataṃ nasappac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

9-22. sanidassanaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

89. nasanidassanaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasanidassanaṃ  naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanau anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

nasanidassanaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

10-22. sappaṭighaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

90. nasappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nasappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| (sabbattha tīṇi|)

nasappaṭighaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naappaṭighaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭighau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

11-22. rūpīduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

91. narūpiṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpī sanidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

narūpiṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpī anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naarūpiṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca rūpī anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

12-22. laukiyaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

92. nalaukiyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nalaukuttaraṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

(gaṇitakaina gaṇaitabbā tīṇi pañhā|)

nalaukiyaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau anidassanasappaṭighau… (tīṇiyaiva pañhā kātabbā)|

nalaukuttaraṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

13-22. kainaciviñ‍ñaiyyaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

93. nakainaci  viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau sanidassanasappaṭighau  ca kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau sanidassanasappaṭighau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| (3)

nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇiyaiva|

nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… tīṇiyaiva| (sabbattha nava|)

nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighassa purimasadisaṃ navapañhaṃ kātabbaṃ| nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighamūlassa navapañhamaiva| haituyā nava, adhipatiyā nava…pai॰… avigatai nava|

14-22. āsavaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

94. naāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanauāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

nauāsavaṃ  naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (purimanayaina tīṇi pañhā)|

nauāsavaṃ  naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

15-22. sāsavaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

95. nasāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naanāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

(nasāsavaṃ  naanidassanasappaṭighamūlassa purimanayaina tīṇi pañhā|)

naanāsavaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

16-22. āsavasampayuttaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

96. naāsavasampayuttaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naāsavavippayuttaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

(naāsavasampayuttanaanidassanasappaṭighamūlai tīṇiyaiva|)

naāsavasampayuttaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

17-22. āsavasāsavadukādi-sanidassanattikaṃ

97. naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

naanāsavañ‍caiva  nanau ca āsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

(naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighamūlaipi purimanayaina tīṇiyaiva|)

naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

(āsavaāsavasampayuttadukai pañhā na labbhati|)

19-22. āsavavippayuttasāsavaduka-sanidassanattikaṃ

98. āsavavippayuttaṃ  nasāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau  sāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

(āsavavippayuttanasāsavanaanidassanasappaṭighamūlaipi purimanayainaiva tīṇi pañhā|)

āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavavippayuttau sāsavau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

20-54-22. sañ‍ñaujanādichagaucchakāni-sanidassanattikaṃ

99. nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nausañ‍ñaujanau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| (tīṇi pañhā|)

100. nauganthaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauganthau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

101. nauaughaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauaughau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

102. nauyaugaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauyaugau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

103. naunīvaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naunīvaraṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

104. nauparāmāsaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauparāmāsau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

55-68-22. mahantaradukādi-sanidassanattikaṃ

105. nasārammaṇaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naanārammaṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

(anidassanasappaṭighai tīṇiyaiva|)

nasārammaṇaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anārammaṇau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

106. naucittaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanaucittaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

107. nacaitasikaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nacaitasikau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

108. nacittasampayuttaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittavippayuttau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

109. nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittavisaṃsaṭṭhau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

110. naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca cittasamuṭṭhānau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (cittasamuṭṭhānarūpainaiva tīṇi, saṃkhittaṃ|)

111. naucittasahabhuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasahabhū sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

112. naucittānuparivattiṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittānuparivattī sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

113. naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

114. naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

115. naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

116. naajjhattikaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhirau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nabāhiraṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhirau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

117. nauupādā nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca upādā sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

118. naupādin‍naṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

69-74-22. upādānadukādi-sanidassanattikaṃ

119. nauupādānaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauupādānau  sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

83-100-22. piṭṭhidukādi-sanidassanattikaṃ

121. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

122. nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

123. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

124. nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

125. nasavitak‍kaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

126. nasavicāraṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avicārau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

127. nasappītikaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasappītikau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

128. napītisahagataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca napītisahagatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

129. nasukhasahagataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nasukhasahagatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

130. naupaikkhāsahagataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naupaikkhāsahagatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

131. nakāmāvacaraṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kāmāvacarau  sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

132. narūpāvacaraṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca narūpāvacarau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

133. naarūpāvacaraṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naarūpāvacarau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

134. napariyāpan‍naṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pariyāpan‍nau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

135. naniyyānikaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyyānikau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

136. naniyataṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

137. nasauttaraṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sauttarau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

138. nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naaraṇaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (anidassanasappaṭighai tīṇiyaiva|)

nasaraṇaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau anidassanaappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyaṃ

nahaitu-naārammaṇapac‍cayā

139. nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau  dhammau uppaj‍jati nahaitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nasaraṇaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati naārammaṇapac‍cayā… tīṇi| nahaituyā aikaṃ, naārammaṇai tīṇi, naadhipatiyā tīṇi…pai॰… nauvigatai tīṇi|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai tīṇi… naārammaṇapac‍cayā haituyā tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

7. pañhāvārau

haitupac‍cayau

140. nasaraṇau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau araṇassa sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naaraṇau nasanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau araṇassa sanidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

haituyā dvai, adhipatiyā dvai…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai dukatikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-haitudukaṃ

1. nakusalaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (2)

nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā nava|

2. nakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu ca abyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu ca abyākatau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

naabyākataṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ nanahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naakusalaṃ nanahaituñ‍ca naabyākataṃ nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanahaituñ‍ca naakusalaṃ nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

1-2. kusalattika-sahaitukadukaṃ

3. nakusalaṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  cha|

4. nakusalaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ  naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca naakusalaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā cha|

1-3. kusalattika-haitusampayuttadukaṃ

5. nakusalaṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| (sahaitukasadisaṃ|)

nakusalaṃ  nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… cha| (ahaitukasadisaṃ, saṃkhittaṃ|)

1-4-6. kusalattika-haitusahaitukādidukāni

6. nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

(naakusalai dvai, naabyākatai dvai| paṭhamaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ, dutiyaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ, tatiyaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ, sabbattha nava pañhā|)

nakusalaṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

7. nakusalaṃ  nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ  nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

8. nakusalaṃ  nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

9. nakusalaṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naakusalaṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naabyākataṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1) (gaṇitakaina tīṇi pañhā|)… haituyā cha|

1-7-13. kusalattika-cūḷantaradukāni

10. nakusalau nasappac‍cayau dhammau kusalassa sappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha| (saṅkhataṃ sappac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

11. nakusalaṃ nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalau naanidassanau dhammau kusalassa anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (nava pañhā kātabbā)|

12. nakusalaṃ  nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ  naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau appaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

13. nakusalaṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ  naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau arūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

14. nakusalaṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (cha  pañhā kātabbā|)

15. nakusalaṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

nakusalaṃ nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

1-14. kusalattika-āsavagaucchakaṃ

16. nakusalaṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

17. nakusalaṃ  nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cha…pai॰… vipākai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai cha|

18. nakusalaṃ  naāsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau āsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ naāsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau āsavavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

19. nakusalaṃ  naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau āsavau caiva sāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ naanāsavañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

20. nakusalaṃ naāsavañ‍caiva naāsavavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ naāsavavippayuttañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau  āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

21. nakusalaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau āsavavippayuttau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-20-54. kusalattika-sañ‍ñaujanādigaucchakāni

22. nakusalaṃ nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

23. nakusalaṃ  nauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

24. nakusalaṃ nauaughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau aughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

25. nakusalaṃ nauyaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau yaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

26. nakusalaṃ naunīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau nīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

27. nakusalaṃ  nauparāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

1-55-92. kusalattika-mahantaradukāni

28. nakusalaṃ nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…pai॰…|

1-93. kusalattika-kāmāvacaradukaṃ

29. nakusalaṃ  nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ nanakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-94. kusalattika-rūpāvacaradukaṃ

30. nakusalaṃ narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-95. kusalattika-arūpāvacaradukaṃ

31. nakusalaṃ  naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ nanaarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  pañ‍ca|

1-96. kusalattika-pariyāpan‍nadukaṃ

32. nakusalaṃ napariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau pariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ napariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau pariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ napariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau pariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ  naapariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau apariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naapariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau apariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai| (akusalamūlai dvai, dukamūlai dvai|)… haituyā cha|

1-97. kusalattika-niyyānikadukaṃ

33. nakusalaṃ naniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ naaniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ naaniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naaniyyānikañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naaniyyānikañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

1-98. kusalattika-niyatadukaṃ

34. nakusalaṃ  naniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau niyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (apariyāpan‍nasadisaṃ cha pañhā)|

nakusalaṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naaniyatañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naaniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naaniyatañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naaniyatañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-99. kusalattika-sauttaradukaṃ

35. nakusalaṃ nasauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  abyākatau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasauttarañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasauttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasauttarañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasauttarañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ naanuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau anuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cha…pai॰… vipākai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai cha|

1-100. kusalattika-saraṇadukaṃ

36. nakusalaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ  naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. vaidanāttika-haitudukaṃ

37. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi (dutiyaṃ gaṇitakaina tīṇi)| haituyā  aikavīsa|

nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai|

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (dvai pañhāyaiva)|

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca… (dvaiyaiva| paṭhamaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ, dutiyaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ, tatiyaṃ gaṇitakaina aikaṃ kātabbaṃ|) haituyā nava|

3-1. vipākattika-haitudukaṃ

38. navipākaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

navipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

navipākaṃ nahaituñ‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

navipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

navipākaṃ nahaituñ‍ca navipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| navipākaṃ nahaituñ‍ca navipākadhammadhammaṃ nahaituñ‍ca  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā aikādasa|

navipākaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca vipākadhammadhammau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

4-1. upādin‍nattika-haitudukaṃ

39. naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhārasa|

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-haitudukaṃ

40. nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

6-11-1. vitak‍kādittikāni-haitudukaṃ

41. nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca savitak‍kasavicārau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pan‍narasa|

42. napītisahagataṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca pītisahagatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

43. nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

44. nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  nava|

45. naācayagāmiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca apacayagāmī haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

46. nasaikkhaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaikkhau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

12-13-1. parittattikadvaya-haitudukaṃ

47. naparittaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca mahaggatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikādasa|

48. naparittārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tairasa|

14-17-1. hīnādittikāni-haitudukaṃ

49. nahīnaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

50. namicchattaniyataṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sammattaniyatau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

51. namaggārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca maggahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā dasa|

52. naanuppan‍nau nanahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nauppādī nanahaitu dhammau uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanuppan‍nau nanahaitu ca nauppādī nanahaitu ca dhammā uppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā tīṇi|

18-19-1. atītattikadvaya-haitudukaṃ

53. naatītau nanahaitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanāgatau nanahaitu dhammau pac‍cuppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naatītau nanahaitu  ca naanāgatau nanahaitu ca dhammā pac‍cuppan‍nassa nahaitussa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| haituyā tīṇi|

54. naatītārammaṇaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca atītārammaṇau haitu  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā sattarasa|

20-21-1. ajjhattattikadvaya-haitudukaṃ

55. naajjhattaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhā haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā|

nabahiddhā nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

56. naajjhattārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhattārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai|

nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca bahiddhārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-1. sanidassanattika-haitudukaṃ

57. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (nahaitu nanahaitu auvattā, tīṇi mūlāni, aikavīsati pañhā kātabbā|)

22-2. sanidassanattika-sahaitukadukaṃ

58. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| haituyā aikavīsa|

22-3-6. sanidassanattika-haitusampayuttādidukāni

59. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau haituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

60. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva nana ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

61. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva nana ca haituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

62. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-7-13. sanidassanattika-cūḷantaradukāni

63. nasanidassanasappaṭighau nasappac‍cayau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa sappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā upanissayai tīṇi|

64. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau sanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

naanidassanaappaṭighau nanasanidassanau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa nasanidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai tīṇi, adhipatiyā upanissayai puraijātai atthiyā avigatai tīṇi|

65. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau appaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

66. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau arūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

67. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

68. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  pañ‍catiṃsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍catiṃsa|

22-14-19. sanidassanattika-āsavagaucchakāni

69. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

70. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai tīṇi|

71. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naāsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau āsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naāsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

72. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau āsavau caiva sāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanāsavañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

73. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naāsavañ‍caiva naāsavavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naāsavavippayuttañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ  paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

74. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau āsavavippayuttau anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-20-54. sanidassanattika-sañ‍ñaujanādigaucchakāni

75. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

76. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau  ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

77. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nauaughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau aughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

78. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauyaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau yaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

79. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naunīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau nīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

80 . nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauparāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau  parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-55-68. sanidassanattika-mahantaradukāni

81. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

82. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

83. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau caitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau acaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

84. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nacittavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau cittavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

85. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nacittavisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau cittavisaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

86. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau cittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau naucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

87. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

88. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naucittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  pañ‍ca|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

89. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

90. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

91. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

92. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanasappaṭighau ajjhattikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikādasa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabāhiraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau bāhirau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

93. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau upādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍catiṃsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau nauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

94. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanupādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau anupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-69-74. sanidassanattika-upādānādidukāni

95. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nauupādānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau upādānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-75-82. sanidassanattika-kilaisādidukāni

96. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naukilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-83-99. sanidassanattika-piṭṭhidukāni

97. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

98. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

99. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|
