nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

100. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

101. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasavitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau avitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

102. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau savicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau avicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

103. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sappītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappītikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau appītikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

104. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nanapītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau napītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

105. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau nasukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  aikavīsa|

106. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nanaupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naupaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

107. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau kāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanakāmāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau nakāmāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

108. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ narūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau rūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau narūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

109. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau arūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaarūpāvacaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naarūpāvacarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

110. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ napariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau pariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naapariyāpan‍naṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau apariyāpan‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

111. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau niyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaniyyānikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau aniyyānikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

112. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau niyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

113. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naanuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau anuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

22-100. sanidassanattika-saraṇadukaṃ

114. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta| haituyā aikavīsa|

(sahajātavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

haituārammaṇapac‍cayādi

115. nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa  araṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau sanidassanasappaṭighassa araṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

nasanidassanasappaṭighau  naaraṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca dhammā sanidassanasappaṭighassa araṇassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… satta|

116. nasanidassanasappaṭighau  naaraṇau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca naanidassanasappaṭighau naaraṇau ca dhammā anidassanaappaṭighassa araṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

117. haituyā aikavīsa, ārammaṇai tīṇi…pai॰… avigatai aikavīsa|

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvārassa anulaumampi pac‍canīyampi anulaumapac‍canīyampi pac‍canīyānulaumampi gaṇitaṃ, aivaṃ gaṇaitabbaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. kusalattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

1. nakusalaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

nakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā nava|

2. nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naabyākataṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā tīṇi|

3. nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalaṃ  naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naabyākataṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kusalau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca naakusalaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā nava|

1-2. kusalattika-vipākattikaṃ

4. nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca naakusalaṃ navipākañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā tīṇi|

5. nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai|

nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca naakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|) haituyā cha pañhā|

6. nakusalaṃ nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-3. kusalattika-upādin‍nattikaṃ

7. nakusalau  naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau abyākatassa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… cha pañhā|

nakusalaṃ  naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ naanupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-4. kusalattika-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

8. nakusalaṃ nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā…| (akusalānaiva tīṇi|)

nakusalaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-5. kusalattika-vitak‍kattikaṃ

9. nakusalaṃ nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ  naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvādasa|

1-6. kusalattika-pītittikaṃ

10. nakusalaṃ napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau upaikkhāsahagatau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-7. kusalattika-dassanattikaṃ

11. nakusalaṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-8. kusalattika-dassanahaituttikaṃ

12. nakusalaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca akusalau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-9. kusalattika-ācayagāmittikaṃ

13. nakusalaṃ naācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ naapacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-10. kusalattika-saikkhattikaṃ

14. nakusalaṃ  nasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau saikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ naasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā abyākatau asaikkhau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-11. kusalattika-parittattikaṃ

15. nakusalaṃ naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nakusalaṃ namahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  naappamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-12. kusalattika-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

16. nakusalaṃ naparittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  namahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ naappamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-13. kusalattika-hīnattikaṃ

17. nakusalaṃ nahīnaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau hīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ namajjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ  napaṇītaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau paṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-14. kusalattika-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

18. nakusalaṃ namicchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā akusalau micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  nasammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

1-15. kusalattika-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

19. nakusalaṃ namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nakusalaṃ namaggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ namaggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

1-16. kusalattika-uppan‍nattikaṃ

20. nakusalau nauppan‍nau dhammau kusalassa uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai  aṭṭhārasa|

1-17-18. kusalattika-atītattikadvayaṃ

21. nakusalau napac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau kusalassa pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa|

22. nakusalaṃ naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā kusalau anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nakusalaṃ napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-19-20. kusalattika-ajjhattattikadvayaṃ

23. nakusalau  naajjhattau dhammau ajjhattassa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa, adhipatiyā sauḷasa, upanissayai aṭṭhārasa, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai nava|

nakusalau nabahiddhā dhammau bahiddhā kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai aṭṭhārasa, adhipatiyā cha, upanissayai aṭṭhārasa, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai nava|

24. nakusalaṃ naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nakusalaṃ  nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-21. kusalattika-sanidassanattikaṃ

25. nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naakusalaṃ  nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naabyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca naakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau sanidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā cha|

26. nakusalaṃ naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  cha|

nakusalaṃ naanidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca abyākatau anidassanasappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. vaidanāttika-kusalattikaṃ

27. nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai|

naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| dvai| (cattāri gaṇitakaina dvai dvai pañhā kātabbā|) haituyā cuddasa|

28. nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau akusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikavīsa|

29. nasukhāya  vaidanāya sampayuttau naabyākatau dhammau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cuddasa|

3-1. vipākattika-kusalattikaṃ

30. navipākaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

navipākaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā vipākadhammadhammau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

navipākaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

4-1. upādin‍nattika-kusalattikaṃ

31. naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anupādin‍nupādāniyau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anupādin‍nupādāniyau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

5-1. saṃkiliṭṭhattika-kusalattikaṃ

32. nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau  abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

6-1. vitak‍kattika-kusalattikaṃ

33. nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pan‍narasa|

nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā savitak‍kasavicārau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca avitak‍kaavicārau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā satta|

7-1. pītittika-kusalattikaṃ

34. napītisahagataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā pītisahagatau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

napītisahagataṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā pītisahagatau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aṭṭhavīsa|

napītisahagatau  naabyākatau dhammau pītisahagatassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai aṭṭhavīsa, adhipatiyā anantarai aṭṭhavīsa…pai॰… upanissayai aṭṭhavīsa, kammai catuvīsa, natthiyā vigatai aṭṭhavīsa|

8-1. dassanattika-kusalattikaṃ

35. nadassanaina  pahātabbaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

9-1. dassanahaituttika-kusalattikaṃ

36. nadassanaina  pahātabbahaitukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  cha|

nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

10-1. ācayagāmittika-kusalattikaṃ

37. naācayagāmiṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

naācayagāmiṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ācayagāmī akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naācayagāmiṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

11-1. saikkhattika-kusalattikaṃ

38. nasaikkhaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saikkhau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nasaikkhaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau akusalau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasaikkhaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

12-1. parittattika-kusalattikaṃ

39. namahaggataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā mahaggatau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

namahaggataṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

naparittaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca parittau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

13-1. parittārammaṇattika-kusalattikaṃ

40. naparittārammaṇaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittārammaṇau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

naparittārammaṇaṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā parittārammaṇau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cuddasa|

naparittārammaṇau naabyākatau dhammau parittārammaṇassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|)… ārammaṇai aikavīsa|

14-1. hīnattika-kusalattikaṃ

41. nahīnaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā majjhimau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

nahīnaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā hīnau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahīnaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca majjhimau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

15-1. micchattaniyatattika-kusalattikaṃ

42. namicchattaniyataṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā sammattaniyatau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

namicchattaniyataṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā micchattaniyatau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

namicchattaniyataṃ  naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca aniyatau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

16-1. maggārammaṇattika-kusalattikaṃ

43. namaggārammaṇaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā maggārammaṇau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍catiṃsa| (naakusalaṃ naabyākataṃ natthi|)

17-18-1. uppan‍nādittikāni-kusalattikaṃ

44. nauppan‍nau nakusalau dhammau uppan‍nassa kusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai satta|

nauppan‍nau naakusalau dhammau uppan‍nassa akusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha|

nauppan‍nau naabyākatau dhammau uppan‍nassa abyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai satta|

(atītattikaṃ uppan‍nattikasadisaṃ|)

19-1. atītārammaṇattika-kusalattikaṃ

45. naatītārammaṇaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā atītārammaṇau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

naatītārammaṇaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā atītārammaṇau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

20-1. ajjhattattika-kusalattikaṃ

46. naajjhattaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bahiddhā kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nabahiddhā nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ajjhattau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

naajjhattaṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā bahiddhā akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nabahiddhā naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ajjhattau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā dvai|

21-1. ajjhattārammaṇattika-kusalattikaṃ

47. naajjhattārammaṇaṃ  nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ajjhattārammaṇau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

naajjhattārammaṇaṃ  naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā ajjhattārammaṇau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cha|

22-1. sanidassanattika-kusalattikaṃ

48. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca naanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañ‍ca dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau kusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naakusalaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau akusalau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naabyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau abyākatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… satta|

(naanidassanasappaṭighanaabyākatamūlāni sattamaiva, dukamūlāni sattamaiva, sabbaṃ aikavīsatimaiva|)

22-2. sanidassanattika-vaidanāttikaṃ

49. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naadukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-3. sanidassanattika-vipākattikaṃ

50. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau vipākau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau vipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nanaivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-4. sanidassanattika-upādin‍nattikaṃ

51. nasanidassanasappaṭighau naupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa upādin‍nupādāniyassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naanupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau anupādin‍nupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanupādin‍naanupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau anupādin‍naanupādāniyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-5. sanidassanattika-saṃkiliṭṭhattikaṃ

52. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-6. sanidassanattika-vitak‍kattikaṃ

53. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasavitak‍kasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau savitak‍kasavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavitak‍kavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau avitak‍kavicāramattau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naavitak‍kaavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau avitak‍kaavicārau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … haituyā aikavīsa|

22-7. sanidassanattika-pītittikaṃ

54. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau pītisahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau sukhasahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naupaikkhāsahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau upaikkhāsahagatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-8. sanidassanattika-dassanattikaṃ

55. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-9. sanidassanattika-dassanahaituttikaṃ

56. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naivadassanaina nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-10. sanidassanattika-ācayagāmittikaṃ

57. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naācayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau ācayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naapacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau apacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-11. sanidassanattika-saikkhattikaṃ

58. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau saikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naasaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau  asaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nanaivasaikkhanāsaikkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-12. sanidassanattika-parittattikaṃ

59. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau parittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau mahaggatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappamāṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau appamāṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-13. sanidassanattika-parittārammaṇattikaṃ

60. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naparittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau parittārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namahaggatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau mahaggatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naappamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau appamāṇārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-14. sanidassanattika-hīnattikaṃ

61. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahīnaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau hīnau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namajjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau majjhimau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  napaṇītaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau paṇītau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

22-15. sanidassanattika-micchattaniyatattikaṃ

62. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  namicchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau micchattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nasammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau sammattaniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanasappaṭighau aniyatau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikavīsa|

22-16. sanidassanattika-maggārammaṇattikaṃ

63. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau maggārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namaggahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau maggahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ namaggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau maggādhipati dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

22-17. sanidassanattika-uppan‍nattikaṃ

64. nasanidassanasappaṭighau nauppan‍nau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa uppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha|

22-18. sanidassanattika-atītattikaṃ

65. nasanidassanasappaṭighau  napac‍cuppan‍nau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa pac‍cuppan‍nassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai cha|

22-19. sanidassanattika-atītārammaṇattikaṃ

66. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naatītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau atītārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naanāgatārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā anidassanaappaṭighau anāgatārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ napac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā … haituyā tīṇi|

22-20-21. sanidassanattika-ajjhattattikadvayaṃ

67. nasanidassanasappaṭighau  naajjhattau dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa ajjhattassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā cha, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai cha|

nasanidassanasappaṭighau nabahiddhā dhammau anidassanaappaṭighassa bahiddhā dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| (saṃkhittaṃ|) ārammaṇai cha, adhipatiyā cha, puraijātai atthiyā avigatai cha|

68. nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ naajjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau ajjhattārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ  nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanaappaṭighau bahiddhārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi pañhāvārampi vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai tikatikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ

1-1. haituduka-sahaitukadukaṃ

1. nahaituṃ  nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca  haitu sahaitukau ca nahaitu sahaitukau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nanahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

haituyā cattāri, ārammaṇai cattāri…pai॰… avigatai cattāri|

2. nanahaitu naahaitukau dhammau nahaitussa ahaitukassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

haituyā aikaṃ, ārammaṇai cha…pai॰… avigatai pañ‍ca| (pañhāvāraṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

3. nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ  naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā tīṇi|

1-2-5. haituduka-haitusampayuttādidukāni

4. nahaituṃ  nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā cattāri|

nahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu haituvippayuttau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

5. nahaituṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

6. nahaituṃ nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

7. nahaituṃ nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

1-6-12. haituduka-cūḷantaradukāni

8. nahaitu  nasappac‍cayau dhammau haitussa sappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai tīṇi| (saṅkhataṃ sappac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

9. nahaituṃ  nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaitu naanidassanau dhammau haitussa anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… ārammaṇai tīṇi|

10. nahaituṃ nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu appaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

11. nahaituṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu arūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

12. nahaituṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā (gaṇitakaina tīṇi|)

nahaituṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

13. nahaituṃ  nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  nava|

nahaituṃ nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-13-18. haituduka-āsavagaucchakāni

14. nahaituṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau ca nahaitu āsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā| tīṇi|

nanahaituṃ  nauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ nauāsavañ‍ca nanahaituṃ nauāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā pañ‍ca|

nahaituṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanahaituṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ nanauāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu nauāsavau ca nahaitu nauāsavau ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanauāsavañ‍ca  nanahaituṃ nanauāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nauāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā pañ‍ca|

15. nahaituṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

16. nanahaituṃ naāsavasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naāsavavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

17. nahaituṃ naāsavañ‍caiva naanāsavañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau caiva sāsavau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nahaituṃ naanāsavañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  pañ‍ca|

18. nahaituṃ  naāsavañ‍caiva naāsavavippayuttañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naāsavavippayuttañ‍caiva nanau ca āsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

19. nahaituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ nasāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu āsavavippayuttau sāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ āsavavippayuttaṃ naanāsavaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu āsavavippayuttau anāsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-19. haituduka-sañ‍ñaujanādigaucchakāni

20. nahaituṃ  nausañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

21. nahaituṃ nauganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

22. nahaituṃ nauaughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu aughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

23. nahaituṃ nauyaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu yaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

24. nahaituṃ naunīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu nīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

25. nahaituṃ nauparāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-54. haituduka-mahantaradukaṃ

26. nahaituṃ  nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naanārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

27. nahaituṃ naucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu cittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaucittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naucittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

28. nahaituṃ  nacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu acaitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

29. nahaituṃ nacittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nacittavippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu cittavippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

30. nahaituṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nacittavisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu cittavisaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

31. nahaituṃ naucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasamuṭṭhānau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaucittasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu naucittasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

32. nahaituṃ  naucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaucittasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

33. nahaituṃ nacittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nanaucittānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

34. nahaituṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

35. nahaituṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

36. nahaituṃ naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanaucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu naucittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

37. nahaituṃ  naajjhattikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu ajjhattikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ  nabāhiraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu bāhirau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

38. nahaituṃ naupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu upādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanauupādā dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu nauupādā dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

39. nahaituṃ  naanupādin‍naṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu anupādin‍nau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-68. haituduka-upādānagaucchakaṃ

40. nahaituṃ naupādānaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu upādānau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

1-74. haituduka-kilaisagaucchakaṃ

41. nahaituṃ nakilaisaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu kilaisau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

1-82. haituduka-piṭṭhidukaṃ

42. nahaituṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

43. nahaituṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu bhāvanāya pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ nanabhāvanāya pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nabhāvanāya  pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

44. nanahaituṃ nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ  nanadassanaina pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nadassanaina pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

45. nanahaituṃ nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituṃ nanabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu nabhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

46. nahaituṃ nasavitak‍kaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu savitak‍kau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi| (sabbattha saṃkhittaṃ|)

47. nahaituṃ nasaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā haitu saraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nahaituṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nanahaituṃ naaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nahaituṃ naaraṇañ‍ca nanahaituṃ naaraṇañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nahaitu araṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

2-1. sahaitukādidukāni-haitudukaṃ

48. nasahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca naahaitukaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

nasahaitukaṃ  nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

naahaitukaṃ  nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca ahaitukau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| haituyā cha|

49. nahaitusampayuttaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitusampayuttau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (sahaitukadukasadisaṃ|)

50. nahaituñ‍caiva  naahaitukaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva sahaitukau ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naahaitukañ‍caiva nana ca haituṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

51. nahaituñ‍caiva nahaituvippayuttañ‍ca nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nahaituvippayuttañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitu nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ| (antimadukaṃ na labbhati|)

7-13-1. cūḷantaradukāni-haitudukaṃ

52. naappac‍cayaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naappac‍cayaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappac‍cayau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ| (saṅkhataṃ sappac‍cayasadisaṃ|)

53. nasanidassanaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca anidassanau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasanidassanaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sanidassanau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā tīṇi|

54. nasappaṭighaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca appaṭighau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

nasappaṭighaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sappaṭighau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

55. narūpiṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpī haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

narūpiṃ  nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca arūpī nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… gaṇitakaina tīṇi|

56. nalaukiyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukuttarau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nalaukuttaraṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā dvai|

nalaukiyaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nalaukuttaraṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca laukiyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā cattāri|

57. nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  nava|

nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā nava|

14-1. āsavagaucchaka-haitudukaṃ

58. nauāsavaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nanauāsavaṃ  nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nauāsavaṃ nahaituñ‍ca nanauāsavaṃ nahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca āsavau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā pañ‍ca|

nauāsavaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

nanauāsavaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi|

nauāsavaṃ  nanahaituñ‍ca nanauāsavaṃ nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca nauāsavau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā pañ‍ca|

55-1. mahantaraduka-haitudukaṃ

59. nasārammaṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā  tīṇi|

naanārammaṇaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca sārammaṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-1. saraṇaduka-haitudukaṃ

60. nasaraṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nasaraṇaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| (1)

naaraṇaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau nahaitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nanahaituṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau nahaitu ca araṇau nahaitu ca dhammā uppaj‍janti haitupac‍cayā… tīṇi| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-2. saraṇaduka-sahaitukadukaṃ

61. nasaraṇaṃ  nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

62. nasaraṇaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau ahaitukau dhammau  uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-3. saraṇaduka-haitusampayuttadukaṃ

63. nasaraṇaṃ  nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nahaitusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau haitusampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

64. nasaraṇaṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nahaituvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haituvippayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-4. saraṇaduka-haitusahaitukadukādi

65. nasaraṇaṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nahaituñ‍caiva naahaitukañ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nasaraṇaṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ naahaitukañ‍caiva nanahaituñ‍ca dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  dvai| (haituhaitusampayuttadukaṃ saṃkhittaṃ|)

100-6. saraṇaduka-nahaitusahaitukadukaṃ

66. nasaraṇaṃ  nahaituṃ nasahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu sahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasaraṇaṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ|

naaraṇaṃ nahaituṃ naahaitukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nahaitu ahaitukau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| aikaṃ| haituyā dvai|

100-7. saraṇaduka-cūḷantaradukaṃ

67. nasaraṇau  nasappac‍cayau dhammau araṇassa sappac‍cayassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇai aikaṃ|

68. nasaraṇau nasaṅkhatau dhammau… (saṃkhittaṃ)|

69. nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sanidassanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

nasaraṇau naanidassanau dhammau saraṇassa anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nasaraṇau naanidassanau dhammau araṇassa anidassanassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| ārammaṇai dvai|

70. nasaraṇaṃ  nasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sappaṭighau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā tīṇi|

71. nasaraṇaṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ narūpiṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau rūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

nasaraṇaṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā saraṇau arūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| nasaraṇaṃ naarūpiṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā araṇau arūpī dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

72. nasaraṇaṃ  nalaukiyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau laukiyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasaraṇaṃ nalaukuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā araṇau laukuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

73. nasaraṇaṃ nakainaci viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau  dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

nasaraṇaṃ nakainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau kainaci naviñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā… haituyā pañ‍ca|

100-14-54. saraṇaduka-āsavagaucchakādi

74. naaraṇaṃ  naāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau āsavau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

75. naaraṇaṃ nasañ‍ñaujanaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau sañ‍ñaujanau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

76. naaraṇaṃ naganthaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau ganthau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

77. naaraṇaṃ naaughaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau aughau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ|

78. naaraṇaṃ nauyaugaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau yaugau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

79. naaraṇaṃ nanīvaraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau nīvaraṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

80. naaraṇaṃ naparāmāsaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau parāmāsau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

100-55-82. saraṇaduka-mahantaradukādi

81. nasaraṇaṃ  nasārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

nasaraṇaṃ naanārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ naanārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau anārammaṇau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

82. nasaraṇaṃ  nacittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau cittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nacittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau cittau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

83. nasaraṇaṃ  nacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau caitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nacaitasikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau caitasikau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

84. nasaraṇaṃ nacittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau cittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nacittasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau cittasampayuttau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

85. nasaraṇaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau cittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| naaraṇaṃ nacittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau cittasaṃsaṭṭhau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā dvai|

100-83. saraṇaduka-piṭṭhidukaṃ

86. nasaraṇaṃ nadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca saraṇau dassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

naaraṇaṃ nanadassanaina pahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau nadassanaina pahātabbau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā  aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

87. nasaraṇaṃ  nasauttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭic‍ca araṇau sauttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ|

(sahajātavārampi pac‍cayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭic‍cavārasadisaṃ|)

pañhāvārau

haitu-ārammaṇapac‍cayā

88. nasaraṇau nasauttarau dhammau araṇassa sauttarassa dhammassa haitupac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ|

nasaraṇau nasauttarau dhammau araṇassa sauttarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| aikaṃ| haituyā aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

89. nasaraṇau  nasauttarau dhammau araṇassa sauttarassa dhammassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… (saṃkhittaṃ|) nahaituyā  aikaṃ, naārammaṇai aikaṃ|

haitupac‍cayā naārammaṇai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaitupac‍cayā ārammaṇai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

anuttarapadaṃ

haitu-anantarapac‍cayā

90. nasaraṇaṃ naanuttaraṃ dhammaṃ pac‍cayā araṇau anuttarau dhammau uppaj‍jati haitupac‍cayā| haituyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ|

91. nasaraṇau  naanuttarau dhammau araṇassa anuttarassa dhammassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| anantarai aikaṃ, samanantarai aikaṃ, upanissayai dvai, puraijātai aikaṃ, āsaivanai aikaṃ, vippayuttai aikaṃ, atthiyā aikaṃ, natthiyā aikaṃ, vigatai aikaṃ, avigatai aikaṃ|

pac‍canīyuddhārau

92. nasaraṇau naanuttarau dhammau araṇassa anuttarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau… puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

naaraṇau naanuttarau dhammau araṇassa anuttarassa dhammassa upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| nahaituyā dvai, naārammaṇai dvai…pai॰… naupanissayai aikaṃ, napuraijātai dvai…pai॰… nauavigatai dvai|

upanissayapac‍cayā nahaituyā dvai| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

nahaitupac‍cayā  upanissayai dvai, puraijātai aikaṃ…pai॰… atthiyā aikaṃ…pai॰… avigatai aikaṃ| (saṃkhittaṃ|)

(yathā kusalattikai pañhāvāraṃ aivaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|)

anulaumadukatikapaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhāya yāva pariyausānā tiṃsamattaihi bhāṇavāraihi paṭṭhānaṃ|

dhammapac‍canīyānulaumai dukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pac‍canīyānulaumapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

paṭṭhānapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
